tirukkuṟaḷaip paṟṟiya palavēṟu amcaṅkaḷaiyum carācarit tamiḻmakaṉ aṟintu koḷḷum vitattil inta nūlil eḷimai paṭac colkiṟār. maṉakkōṭṭamillāmal vaḷḷuvattaik kaṟka itil vaḻi kāṭṭukiṟār. tirukkuṟaḷaip paṟṟip paravi niṟkum - parappappaṭum kuṟukiya moḻi, iṉa veṟik koḷkaikaḷai varalāṟṟu nōkkōṭum, teḷinta ilakkiya aṟivōṭum piṭṭup piṭṭuk kāṭṭi ampalap paṭuttukiṟār 
tolkāppiyam, pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭuttokai, patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku, tirukkuṟaḷ, kamparāmāyaṇam, cilappatikāram - eṉṟa tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ākac ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅ kaḷaic cātāraṇa makkaḷukku aṟimukappaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṉum nōkkōṭu āyntu, ciṟanta nūlkaḷai varicaiyākat tīṭṭittantatu cātaṉaiyākum intac cātaṉai itu varai yārum cātikkāta perum cātaṉai itaippōkap pōkattāṉ tamiḻaṉ uṇarvāṉ eṉpatil aiyamillai
eḻuttu (pipravari 1961) ilakkiya māta itaḻil tiṟaṉāyvāḷar ci.cu. cellappā vaḻaṅkiya pukaḻuraikaḷ ivai itutāṉ āṅkilattil cōṣiyālajikal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. ilakkiya campantamāka camutāyap pārvai pārttu takavalkaḷai veḷik koṇarum oru vimarcaṉa muṟai vaḻiākum. carittira paṇpāṭṭu rītiyāṉa pār vakaḷ kalantum kūṭa, caṅka kālak kavitaikaḷ mutal pārati varai anēkamāka ellā paḻaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum avar inta nōkkil pārttu, ārāyntu, racamāṉa, iṉpamāṉa upayōkamāṉa pala takavalkaḷai aṟivit tirukkiṟār- ilakkiyam mūlam teriyavarum oru nākarīka paṇpāṭṭu vāḻvu paṟṟiya pārvaiyai maṭṭum āḻntu celutti, uyarnta aḷavu takavalkaḷai ovvoru nūlilum veḷikkāṭṭi iruppavar cāmi.citamparaṉār.
ilaṅkai eḻuttāḷar ec.em.pi.mukaitīṉ vaḻaṅkiya pukaḻuraikaḷ ivai;
citamparaṉāriṉ tamiḻppaṇi palatarappaṭṭavai eṉṟālum avaratu tamiḻt toṇṭiṉ imayacikaramākac cuṭar viṭṭup pirakācippatu avaratu paṇṭaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāy vākum. intat tuṟaiyil amarar citamparaṉāriṉ aruntamiḻt toṇṭu pēraṟiñarkaḷ u.vē. cāmināta aiyariṉatum e. vaiyāyurip piḷḷaiyiṉatum tamiḻp paṇiyuṭaṉ nikar niṟkak kūṭiyatu. eṉṟālum ivviru tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ talaiciṟanta ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvut tilakaṅkaḷaiyum cila amcaṅkaḷil cāmi. citamparaṉār miñci viṭṭiruntār. citamparaṉāriṉ tiṟaṉāyvilē oru putuk kaṇṇōṭṭam maṉitaṉai vāḻa vaikkum kaṇṇōṭṭam  maṉitaṉukku nal vāḻvu tuvajattai tūkkippiṭittu veṟṟinaṭai pōṭa ātarcam nalkum kaṇṇōṭṭam palkip paravi iruntatu. inta ulakap pārvai citamparaṉārai avarukku muṉṉāl paṇṭaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷait tiṟaṉāyvu naṭatti tamiḻukku arum perum toṇṭāṟṟiya aṟiñarkaḷiliruntu vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭiyatuṭaṉ, tamiḻ ilakkiyat tuṟaiyilē avarkaḷukku illāta oru taṉipperum tāṉattaic citamparaṉārukkup peṟṟuttantatu
cāmi. citamparaṉāriṉ cila karuttukkaḷ ārāycci aṟiñar vaiyāpurip piḷḷaiyai aṭiyoṟṟic celvaṉa.
tiṟaṉāyvāl citamparaṉār kaṇṭu collum karuttukkaḷ cilavaṟṟai piṟa aṟiñarkaḷ oppuk koḷḷa muṭiyāviṭṭālum, avai avarkaḷuṭaiya cintaṉaiyaik kiṇṭik kiḷaṟi viṭum vaṇṇam viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatil tuḷiyumaiyamillai.
tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷāṉa ṭākṭar mu. varatarācaṉār ki.vā, jekannātaṉ ākiyōrum caṅka ilakkiyattaic cātāraṇa māṉavarkaḷum paṭittuṇarattakka vakaiyil, ilakkiya nayattuṭaṉ, mika eḷiya naṭaiyil viḷakka nūlkaḷāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar,
cāmi. citamparaṉāruṭaiya caṅka ilakkiyam paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, ivarkaḷiṉiṉṟum muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭu, āyvuk kaṇṇēṭṭam uṭaiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
pērāciriyar cāmi.citamparaṉār vāḻaiyaṭi vāḻaiyeṉa vantuḷḷa tamiḻp pulavar marapiṉ vaḻittōṉṟal; tamiḻ nūlkaḷai muṟaiyākak kaṟṟup pulamai peṟṟavarkaḷiṉ tokai aruki varum innāḷil antac cilaril oruvarākac ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya mūtaṟiñar camutāya māṟutalukkāka iyakkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṇaintu niṉṟu paḻaiyaṉa kaḻittup putiyaṉa pukuttip paṇ pāṭṭiṉaic cemmai ceyyat toṇṭāṟṟiya tamiḻperiyār
cintippataṟkup pōtumāṉa nēramum, vacatiyum uḷḷavar kaḷē ciṟanta nūlkaḷai ākka muṭiyum. attakaiya nēramum. vacatiyum citamparaṉār taṉ vāḻnāḷil peṟṟiruntār.
piṟkālattil tamiḻ ilakkiya cuvaiyil muṟṟilun tōyntuviṭṭa pērāciriyar cāmi. citamparaṉār avarkaḷ aruḷneṟit toṭar varicaiyil āṟu puttakaṅkaḷukkumēl eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ atu tavira mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷ mikak kaṭumaiyāṉa uḻaippiṉai mēṟkoṇṭu patiṉāyiram pāṭal kaḷuṭaiya kamparāmāyaṇattiliruntu, ilakkiyattaram atikam vāynta 3949 pāṭalkaḷait tērnteṭuttu, kamparāmāyaṇat tokuppu oṉṟai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
kampaṉiṭamuḷḷa īṭupāṭṭaiyum avarē vivarikkiṟār.
kampa nāṭaṉ kavitaiyiṟ pōl
 kaṟṟōrkku itayam kaḷiyātē
eṉpatu oru paḻantamiḻp pulavar pāṭṭu. kampaṉ kaviyaip paṭittavarkē ataṉ arumai teriyum. tamiḻiṉ arumaiyai aṟintavar iṉimaiyai uṇarntavar, kampaṉ kavitaiyilē īṭupaṭṭāl kavalaikaḷaiyellām maṟantu viṭuvar kaḷippuk kaṭalilē nīnti viḷaiyāṭuvar; uḷḷattilē kaviccuvaiyaip pāycci kaḷippūṭṭuvatilē kampaṉ kavikkunikar kampaṉ kaviyētāṉ. mēlē kāṭṭiya paḻantamiḻp pāṭṭiṉ pakuti ivvuṇmaiyai uṇarttum! (kampaṉ perumai, kampaṉ kaṇṭa tamiḻakam 1955)
itupōṉṟē āḻvārkaḷiṉ pāṭalil uḷḷamtōynta citamparaṉār āḻvārkaḷ kaṇṭa aruḷneṟi eṉṉum nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil kūṟukiṟār.
ciṟappāka, āḻvārkaḷiṉ aruntamiḻp pāṭalkaḷ paṭippōr uḷḷattaik kavarum paṇpuṭaiyavai karuttil maṭṭum aṉṟu; tamiḻ naṭaiyilum ciṟantavai; iṉiya tamiḻil amaintavai; pakti eṉpatu mūṭa nampikkai eṉṟu kūṟu vōrum āḻvārkaṉiṉ pāṭalkaḷaip paṭikkum pōtu, appāṭalkaḷiṉ iṉimaiyai aṉupavikkāmal irukka māṭṭārkaḷ āṉāl ciṟitaḷavāvatu tamiḻaṟivum tamiḻp paṟṟum vēṇṭum ivai uḷḷavarkaḷāltāṉ entat tamiḻp pāṭalaiyum cuvaikka muṭiyum
iḷamaik kālattiliruntē nāttikarākavum, pakut taṟivāḷarākavum viḷaṅki, cuyamariyātai iyakkattilum; tirāviṭar kaḻakattilum īṭupāṭu koṇṭa tamiḻp perum pulavar pērāciriyar cāmi. citamparaṉār avarkaḷ kampaṉiṭattilum, āḻvārkaḷiṭattilum koṇṭuḷḷa īṭupāṭu muraṇpāṭākap palarukkut tōṉṟak kūṭum.
ataiyum cāmi. citamparaṉār. āḻvārkaḷ kaṇṭa aruḷneṟi eṉṉum nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil viḷakkukiṟār.
nāṉ pakti mārkkattil īṭupaṭṭavaṉ allaṉ; mata paktaṉum allaṉ; nāṉ enta matattaip piṉpaṟṟukiṟēṉ eṉṟu eṉakkēt teriyātu. āṉāl uṇmai aṉpu  oḻukkam aṟivu camattuvam ivaṟṟai virumpukiṟēṉ yāriṭattilum ettakaiya pētamum pāraṭṭāmal ellōrum oṉṟupaṭṭu, pakti mārkkattil īṭupaṭṭiruppōr pāṭalaip pōṟṟukiṟēṉ; paṭikkiṟēṉ; cuvaikkiṟēṉ. itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉ koḷkaiyai oṭṭiya karuttukkaḷai appāṭalilē kāṇkiṉṟēṉ.
itē karuttai cāmi. citamparaṉār vēṟōru iṭattilum valiyuṟuttik kūṟiyuḷḷār.
eṉṉai oru vaitīkaṉ; matavāti; ātikaṉ eṉṟu coṉṉāl atai nāṉ oppukkoḷḷamāṭṭēṉ. eṉṉai oru nātikaṉ mata nampikkaiyillātavaṉ eṉṟu coṉṉāl veṭkappaṭa māṭṭēṉ; maṟukkamāṭṭēṉ (muṉṉurai. vaḷḷuvar kāṭṭiya vaitikam 1960)
1924 mutal 1940 varai, patiṉāṟu āṇṭukaḷ, tamiḻāciri yārākap paṇiyāṟṟi māṇavarkaḷukkut tamiḻaṟivu ūṭṭiyum 1924 mutal 1948 varai irupattu nāṉkāṇṭukaḷ araciyal camutāyam, cīrtiruttam, ilakkiyam eṉṟu palvēṟu pirivu kaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟiyum vanta cāmi. citamparaṉār. 1948kkup piṟaku tam karuttu muḻuvataiyum ilakkiyat tuṟaiyilēyē celuttiṉār. 1948 mutal avar maṟainta 1961 varai paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷukkum mēlāka, ilakkiya ārāycciyil īṭupaṭṭār. ciṅkappūr tamiḻ muracu itaḻiṉ mūṉṟu pakutikaḷukku ikkāla muḻuvatum vārattukku mūṉṟu kaṭṭuraikaḷ vītam iṭai yaṟāmal eḻuti aṉuppiṉār. pārāṭṭu talkaḷ kuvintaṉ ivaratu pukaḻum perumaiyum paraviṉa itaṉāl iṉpamum maṉa niṟaivum peṟṟu mey maṟantu uḻaittār. ippaṇitāṉ taṉakkup piṟkālattil nilaiyāṉa pukaḻaittēṭit tarum eṉṟu nampiṉār. etirkālat tamiḻ māṇavarkaḷ tam paṇiyiṉāl naṟpayaṉ peṟuvareṉṟu collic colli makiḻntār.
1961 caṉavari mātattil poṅkal camayattil tiṭīreṉṟu vantu tākkiya pakkavāta nōyāl āṟu nāṭkaḷ allaluṟṟu, 17.01.1961-l iyaṟkai eytiṉār.
cīrtiruttac cemmal cāmi. citamparaṉārukkuk kālvaḻic cēykaḷ illai eṉpatu uṇmai; āṉāl aṟupatukkum mēṟpaṭṭa nūlvaḻic cēykaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avai avaratu pukaḻmikka perumaiyait tamiḻulakil nilaikkac ceyyum eṉpatu uṟuti.
tamiḻp pērāciriyar tamiḻpperumpulavar tamiḻ ārāycciyāḷar, tamiḻ ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvāḷar tamiḻp pāṭa nūlāciriyar tamiḻp pāvalar cāmi. citamparaṉāriṉ tiruppukaḻ vāḻka!

nilaviya cīrkarantai nīḷnakara vītiyilē
ceṅkatirōṉ tōṉṟic civanta katirparappi
eṅkuviḻi vaittālum epporuḷum poṉmayamāyk

vāḻtturai
ṭākṭar kalaiñar māṇpumiku
mu.karuṇāniti
tamiḻaka mutalvar.
tamiḻaṟiñar kaviñar cāmi. citamparaṉār avarkaḷiṉ kavitait tokuppu nūlāciya aṇainta viḷakku peyaraḷavil aṇainta viḷakkāka iruntālum, uṇmaiyil itu aṇaiyā viḷakku eṉpatai itaṉaip paṭikkiṉṟa aṉaivarum naṉku uṇarvārkaḷ. iṉṟaiya talaimuṟaiyiṉarkkuc cāmi.citam paraṉār avarkaḷiṉ peyar avvaḷavāka aṟimukamākiyirukka niyāyamillai. tantai periyār avarkaḷaip paṟṟi antak kālattilēyē arumaiyāṉa oru varalāṟṟu nūlai tamiḻar talaivar eṉṟa talaippil ariya ilakkiyamāka ākkiyavar tamiḻaṟiñar cāmi.citamparaṉār āvār.
puraṭcik kaviñar pāratitācaṉ avarkaḷuṭaiya mutal kavitai nūl veḷivantapōtu, ataṟku aṇinturai vaḻaṅkip puraṭcik kaviñaraiyē aṟimukappaṭuttiyavar kaviñar citamparaṉār āvār. avarum avartam tuṇaiviyār tirumati civakāmi citamparaṉārum, kalapput tirumaṇam purintu, antak kālatti lēyē puraṭcikaramāṉa ilaṭciyat tampatikaḷāka pakuttaṟivup pācaṟaiyil viḷaṅkiyavarkaḷ.
caṅka ilakkiyattiṟku nikarāṉa intap putumai ilakkiyattai iṉṟaiyat tamiḻulakam nallātaravu nalki varavēṟkum eṉṟu tiṭamāka nampukiṉṟēṉ.

āyvurai
makāvittuvāṉ
mē.vī.vēṇukōpālap piḷḷai
āciriyar cāmi.citamparaṉār kalaitēruḷḷam vāyntavar. tam nuṇṇaṟivāl nūl pala āyntu cila nūlkaḷukku urai yeḻutiyatuṭaṉ nalla karuttukaḷaṭaṅkiya nūl cilavaṟṟait tāmē iyaṟṟiyum veḷiyiṭṭavar. avartam pulamai pōṟṟu taṟkuriyatu.
avar eḻuti vaittuc ceṉṟa nūlkaḷuḷ itukāṟum accēṟāka nūlkaḷuḷ aṇainta viḷakku eṉṉum ic ceyyuḷ nūlum oṉṟu. itaṉai avar tam illattaraciyār tiruvāṭṭiyār civakāmiyammaiyār ollum vakai muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭiruppatu peritum pārāṭṭattakka ceyalākum.
nūliṉ ceyyuḷ naṭai kaṟpārkku iṉpam payattaṟkuriya tāy amaintuḷḷatu. āciriyarē innūlaip patippittirun tāl, nūl piṉṉum peruñciṟappeytiyirukkum; patippu muṟai mēlum ceppamuṟṟirukkum.
icciṟu kāppiyattiṟkuk karuvāyamainta katai, aim peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāyt tikaḻntu maṟainta kuṇṭala kēciyiṉ kataiyākum. maṟainta nūlukku maṟu vāḻvaḷikka viḻaintu āciriyar mēṟkoṇṭa naṉ muyaṟciyiṉ payaṉē innūl.
ceynnaṉṟi maṟavāc centamiḻc cemmakkaḷ itaṉai ollum vakai utavip paravacceyvatait tam kaṭaṉākak koḷvārkaḷāyiṉ, aṭuttu varum patippu piṉṉum ciṟap ceytuvatuṭaṉ, maṟainta karuntaṉattai mīṇṭum peṟṟu makiḻvatu pōṉṟa iṉpattai innūl vāyilākat tamiḻakam peṟum eṉpatu uṟuti.
tamiḻaṟiñar cāmi citamparaṉār pukaḻai icciṟukāppiyam kuṉṟiṉ mītiṭṭa viḷakkup pōlak kulavac ceyyum eṉpatu eṉatu karuttu.
vāḻka citamparaṉār tam vaḷarpuka ḻeṉa vāḻttu kiṉṟēṉ!

aṇinturai
uyartiru ne.tu.cuntaravaṭivēlu
M.A.,L.T. avarkaḷ
tuṇaivēntar, ceṉṉai palkalaikkaḻakam
eḷiya iṉiya tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷ pala iyaṟṟi aḻiyāppukaḻ aṭaintavar cāmi citamparaṉār. ivarkaḷuṭaiya kavitai nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya aṇainta viḷakku ippōḻutu mīṇṭum cuṭarviṭum pēṟu peṟṟiruppatu peru makiḻccikkuriya tēyākum. paḻaiya kuṇṭalakēci kāppiyattait taḻuviya kataic ceyyuḷāy amaintatu aṇainta viḷakku. āciriyar 1. cintiya kaṇmalar; 2. karukiya kañcam; 3. āccariyap pēriruḷ; 4. pālaivaṉa maḻai; 5. coṉṉatē caṭṭam; 6. pūṉai kaiyiṟ kiḷi; 7. kuṟṟamuḷḷa neñcu; 8. kacanta vāḻvu; 9. ūr vāykku mūṭiyillai eṉṉum oṉpatu talaippukaḷil kaliveṇpā, āciriya viruttam, cintu, paḵṟoṭai veṇpā; āciriyappā mutaliya pala pāvakaikaḷālum innūlai yāttuḷḷār.
cāmi citamparaṉār eḷiya iṉiya urainaṭaiyil maṭṭumē vallavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇivantavarkaḷukku ōr iṉpa atircciyāy viḷaṅkuvatu innūl. cāmi citamparaṉār tamatu vaṇṇat tamiḻ pulamaiyāl iṉiya tamiḻkkavikaḷ taravallavar eṉpataṟku aṇaiyā viḷakkam aṇainta viḷakku.
aṇainta viḷakkiṉ peruñciṟappu muṭiyaracu tolaintu kuṭiyaracu amaivatēyākum. ataṉiṉum peruñ ciṟappu kaḷḷaṉaik kātalittu kaṇavaṉākak koṇṭa iḷavaraci avaṉaik koṉṟapiṉ akkuṭiyaracaiyum potuvuṭaimai aracāka māṟṟa muṉaivatēyākum.
cāmi citamparaṉār. centamiḻp paṟṟil ciṟantavar eṉpa taṟkuc ciṟantatōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu kātal moḻiyilum varum kaṉṉittamiḻiṉ pukaḻēyākum. eṭuttukkāṭṭu:
centamiḻt tēṉūṟṟum ceñcoṟ kaviyāṉa
antac cilappatikāram aḷikkiṉṟa
cintāmaṇiyē! ciṟantamaṇi mēkalaiyē!
muntait tamiḻmaṟavar pōrvīram muṟṟum
iṟavāmal meyppulavar iṉcollāṟ ceyta
puṟanāṉūṟē! pukaḻ patiṟppattē eṉ
akanāṉūṟē! naṟṟiṇaiyē! yaruñcoṟ
pukalum kuṟuntokaiyē! aiṅkuṟu nūṟē! nal
paripāṭalē! tamiḻar paṇṭaināḷ māṇpum
ariyatamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ amainta nila vaḷamum
muttumuttāka muḻutun toṭutturaikkum
pattuppāṭṭē! eṉ paḻantamiḻiṉ tēṉperukkē!
ellāp poruḷum itaṉpāluḷ vitaṉpāl
illāta epporuḷum illaiyāl eṉṟu pukal
poyyā moḻiyār pukaṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷiṉ
meyyāṉa mupporuḷē! muttamiḻiṉ melloliyē
uṉṉālē yaṉṟō uyirvāḻvēṉ nāṉulakil
maṉṉavaṉē! eṉṟaṉ maṉampaṟitta mārutamē!
aṇainta viḷakkiṉ āciriyar tammuṭaiya nūliṉ talaiviyai iḷavaraciyai nūl palavum kaṟṟaṟintāḷ. nuṭpa matiyuṭaiyāḷ eṉṟu pōṟṟukiṟār. appaṭippaṭṭavaḷ ārāyāmal oru kaḷḷaṉaik kātalippatu ēṉō? nūl pala kaṟṟālum nuṇṇaṟivu peṟṟālum kāmattiṟkuk kaṇ illai pōlum! ataṉ viḷaivākap paṭṭaṟintē pakuttaṟivāḷiyum payaṉpeṟa vēṇṭum pōlum.
eḷiya iṉiya tamiḻcceyyuḷ iyaṟṟutalilum vallava rāy viḷaṅkum cāmi citaramparaṉār putiya uvamaikaḷaip pukal vatilum kālattai etiroli ceykiṟār eṉpatu teḷivu. ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu :
kaiyāl tirukiyatum kaṭṭuṇṭa taṇṇīraip
peyyum kuḻāypōlap peṇṇamutu taṉkaiyaik
kaṇkaḷilē vaittuk kacakkiyatum tāraiyāy
maṇmītu nīrcinta niṉṟu maṉaṅkuḻaintāḷ.
maṟainta cāmi.citamparaṉār maṟaiyāta tamiḻp pukaḻukku eppoḻutum uriyavar. inta aṇainta viḷakku. ataṟku ōr aṇaiyā viḷakku āvatāka!

ciṟappurai
ṭākṭarci.pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ,
em.ē., em.liṭ., piec.ṭi.,
tamiḻttuṇaip pērāciriyar,
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakam.
tamiḻaṟiñar tiru.cāmi citamparaṉār tamiḻiṟku nilaiyāṉa toṇṭuceytu maṟaintavar; tām iyaṟṟiya takuti vāynta nūṟkaḷiṉ vāyilāka iṟantum iṟavātu vāḻpavar. pakuttaṟivuk koḷkaiyuṭaṉ, ciṟanta kuṟikkōḷōṭu tamiḻ vaḷarccikkāka allum pakalum uḻaittavar. avaruṭaiya eḻutukōl tamiḻiṉ pala tuṟaikaḷaiyum paṟṟi niṉṟatu; payaṉuṟa eḻutiyatu. avartam paṭaippukkaḷil avartam ariya uḻaippu muyaṟciyiṉaiyum, uyariya uṇmaiyāṉa ārāyccip pōkkiṉaiyum oruṅkē kāṇalām. caṅka ilakyi māyiṉum cari, iṭaikkāla pakti ilakkiyamāyiṉum cari piṟkāla ilakkiyamāyiṉum cari, avartam eḻutukōl naṭuvu nilaimaiyuṭaṉ uṇmaikaḷai ārāyntu kaṇṭatu. ciṟanta ilakkiya āciriyariṭam kuṭi koṇṭirukka vēṇṭiya paṇpukaḷ aṉaittum avarkaḷpāl kuṟaivaṟa nirampiyiruntaṉa. avartam uyariya uḻaippum viḻumiya toṇṭum tamiḻiḷaiñar pōṟṟi mēṟkoḷḷat takkavaiyākum.
aṟiñar tiru. cāmi ciramparaṉār avarkaḷ iyaṟṟi, itu varai veḷivarāta aṇainta viḷakku eṉṉum kataic ceyyuḷ ippōtu veḷivarukiṉṟatu.
aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa kuṇṭalakēciyiṉ kataiyiṉai appaṭiyē eṭuttukkoṇṭu, perumpālum kaliveṇpāvilum, ciṟupāṉmai cintu aciriya viruttam āciriyappaḵṟoṭai veṇpā mutaliya yāppu naṭaikaḷilum āciriyar ik kavitai nūliṉai yāttuḷḷār.
nūl, cintiya kaṇmalar, karukiya kañcam, āccariyap pēriruḷ, pālaivaṉa maḻai, coṉṉatē caṭṭam, pūṉai kaiyiṟ kiḷi, kuṟṟamuḷḷa neñcu, kacanta vāḻvu, ūr vāykku mūṭi yillai eṉṟu oṉpatu pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu in nūliṉuṭ ceṉṟu nayaṅkaṇṭu teḷivōm.
kavimaṇi avarkaḷ,
uḷḷat tuḷḷatu kavitai - iṉpam
uruveṭuppatu kavitai
teḷḷat teḷinta tamiḻil - uṇmai
terinturaippatu kavitai
eṉṟu kuyippiṭṭiruppataṟkēṟpa āciriyar kavitai, nūliṉ palaviṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷattu uṇarccikaḷait teḷḷat teḷiyak koṇṭu teḷinta nīrōṭṭam pōl celkiṉṟatu. uṇarcci vēkamum. kaṟpaṉai nayamum, karuttuvaḷamum, vaṭivac ciṟappuṅkoṇṭu kavitai vīṟārntu niṟkiṉṟatu.
āciriyariṉ varuṇaṉait tiṟam, ucciyilē kārmēkam vāḻa uṭampiṉilē paccaic civappu mayila mañcaṇiṟap paṭṭuṭuttik kātilē tōṭaṇintu kaiyil vaḷaitarittu vīti viḷakkēpōl vīcum paṇi pūṇṭu miṉṉal koṭipalavum mētiṉiyil vantulavik kaṉṉal moḻipeytu kātaṟ payir vaḷarttu niṉṟu naṭappaveṉa, nēriḻaiyār paṟpalarum oṉṟu kuḻumiṉār. (pa.61)
eṉṟu peṇkaḷai varuṇikkum pakutiyāl veḷippaṭu kiṉṟatu.
uvamai yaḻaku,
viṇmīṉ puṭaicūḻa veḷḷai nilāvīcit
taṇmatiyam oṉṟu tavampukuntu niṟpatupōl
kārikaiyāḷ niṉṟutaṉ kaṇmalarkaḷ vīcuvatai
vīraṉun tāṉkaṇṭāṉ	(pa:64)
veppattiṟ paṭṭurukum veṇṇeyaip pōluḷḷam
ippaṭi nainturukac ceytāḷ, eḻilaraci	(pa:66)
nīrpukuntu pāluḷḷē niṉṟu maṟaintatupōl
vīraṉavaṉ eṉṉuḷ viraivāyk kalantuviṭṭāṉ	(pa:89)
mutaliya pakutikaḷil viḷakkamuṟukiṉṟatu.
maṇamakkaḷai vāḻttum makkaḷ,
kātalarkaḷ nīṭuḻi vāḻntu kaḷippuṟuka!
ētum kuṟaiviṉṟi iṉpak kaṭalpaṭika!
makkaṭ perumpēṟṟai eyti makiḻvuṭaṉē
ekkālum vāḻka	(pa:116)
eṉa vāḻttukiṉṟaṉar. vāḻttiyalil kaviñar marapaip pōṟṟak kāṇalām.
iḷavaraci cuntaraṉaip pukaḻumpōtu tamiḻpperu nūlkaḷōṭu uvamittup pēcutal āciriyartam tamiḻppaṟṟai viḷakkuvatākum. (pa. 121, 122)
paḻamoḻikaḷai āciriyar iṭaiyiṭaiyē kaiyāḷumtiṟam
kuṟṟamuḷār neñcu kuṟukuṟukkumāmaṟṟu	(pa:126)
kāmālaik kaṇṇaṉukkuk kaṇṭavelām mañcaṇiṟam	(pa:128)
eṉṟa aṭikaḷāl viḷaṅkum.
tirukkuṟaḷaip pala viṭaṅkaḷil appaṭiyē akaḻnteṭuttu aḻakuṟak kaiyāḷukiṉṟār (64, 136)
iṟutiyil iḷavaraci,
maṉṟil uṟaivīr! matiyuṭaiyīr kēṇmiṉō!
iṉṟu mutalāka yāṉum iṉiya tamiḻt
teṉṟal vaḷarntōṅkat teḷḷamutam nāṭellām
ōṭuṟavē ceyvēṉ! ulakellām centamiḻai
nāṭumpaṭiceyvēṉ! nāḷum itaṟkuḻaippēṉ! 	(pa:146)
eṉṟu kūṟuvatu, āciriyar koṇṭuḷḷa uḷḷak kiṭakkai yākum.
kavitai vaḷam niṟainta eḻil nūl aṇainta viḷakku
itaṉai veḷiyiṭum aṉṉaiyām tirumati civakāmi citamparaṉār avarkaḷ tam āruyirk kaṇavar niṉaivu pōṟṟi, paṇpu pōṟṟi, toṇṭupōṟṟi vāḻpavar. aṉṉār viṭṭuc ceṉṟa paṇikaḷait toṭarntu iṭaiyaṟātu iyaṟṟivarupavar. avar tūya paṇi vāḻka, velka eṉa vāḻttuvaṉ.
tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakam ivviṉiya kavitai nūlai vāḻtti varavēṟṟu ilakkiyap payaṉ tuykkumāka!
tamiḻakam	ci.pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ
ceṉṉai 29 	8.10.1973

muṉṉurai
katait tōṟṟam
nīṇṭa nāḷāka eṉakkōr āval uṇṭu. oru kataic ceyyuḷ eḻutavēṇṭum eṉpatē atu. anta āvaliṉ eḻucciyē aṇainta viḷakku, intak katai muḻuvatum eṉatu kaṟpaṉaiyaṉṟu. paḻaṅkataiyum, eṉatu kaṟpaṉaiyum kalantuḷḷa kataiyē itu. ik kataikku aṭippaṭai kuṇṭalakēci; atu paḻaṅkatai.
aimperum kāppiyaṅkaḷ cilappatikāram, cintāmaṇi, maṇimēkalai, kuṇṭalakēci, vaḷaiyāpati eṉpavai. ivaṟṟuḷ kuṇṭala kēciyum oru kāppiyam. āṉāl it tamiḻnūl ippoḻutu kiṭaikka villai it tamiḻk kāppiyattiṉ uṇmaik kataiyum iṉṉa teṉṟu uṇaramuṭiyavillai. pāḷi moḻiyil uḷḷa tēri kātai eṉṉum nūlilum. tamma pātā; aṅkuttaraniyākā eṉṉum nūlkaḷilum kuṇṭala kēciyiṉ katai kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāka moḻikiṉṟaṉar. camaṇacamaya nūlākiya nīlākēci eṉṉum nūlil 286 ām ceyyuḷiṉ uraiyilum kuṇṭalakēciyiṉ katai kūṟap paṭṭirukkiṟatu
irācakirukattu maṉṉaṉatu amaiccaṉukku oru makaḷ. avaḷ peyar pattirai. avaḷ takka paruvamuṟṟāḷ. oru nāḷ taṉ māḷikaiyiṉ māṭiyiṉ mēl ulavikkoṇṭiruntāḷ. aracaṉatu kolaiyāḷikaḷ oru kaṭṭaḻakaṉaik kolaik kaḷattiṟku aḻaittuc ceṉṟaṉar. atai avaḷ kaṇṭāḷ. avaṉ mītu kātaluṟṟāḷ. avaṉ vaḻippaṟi ceyta kuṟṟattiṟkāka maṉṉaṉāl kolait taṇṭaṉai vitikkappaṭṭavaṉ; aracaṉuṭaiya purōkitaṉ makaṉ.
avaṉaiyē maṇakkavēṇṭum eṉṟu pattirai piṭivātam ceytāḷ. amaiccaṉ, kolaiyāḷikaḷukkuk kaikkūli koṭuttāṉ, tiruṭaṉai uyir piḻaikkac ceytāṉ.
kaḷvaṉukkup pattiraiyai maṇamuṭittuk koṭuttāṉ tiruṭaṉum pattiraiyum palanāḷ iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntaṉar. orunāḷ ūṭaliṉ pōtu, pattirai, muṉpu nī kaḷvaṉ aṉṟō eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. avaṉ avaḷ taṉṉai ikaḻntatāka eṇṇic ciṉaṅkoṇṭāṉ. āyiṉum appoḻutu avaṉ ciṉattai veḷiyiṭātu maṟaittu vāḻntiruntāṉ.
orunāḷ avaṉ, pattiraiyiṭam eṉ uyiraik kāppāṟṟiya teyvattai vaṇaṅka, aṇmaiyilirukkum malaimukaṭṭiṟkuc celkiṟēṉ. nīyumvaruka eṉṟāṉ avaḷum uṭaṉpaṭṭāḷ. iruvarum malaiyucciyai aṭaintaṉar. aṭaintatum tiruṭaṉ, nī eṉṉaik kaḷvaṉeṉṟu ikaḻntāy aṉṟō! ātalāl iṉṟu uṇṉaik kollap pōkiṟēṉ uṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvattai vaṇaṅkikoḷ eṉṟāṉ. avaḷ tiṭukkuṟṟāḷ. taṟkolliyai muṟkolla vēṇṭum eṉpatai uḷḷattil eṇṇiṉāḷ. avaṉ āṇaiyai ēṟṟavaḷ pōl naṭittāḷ. uṉṉaiyaṉṟi eṉakkut teyva millai; uṉṉaiyē valam vantu  vaṇaṅkuvēṉ eṉṟāḷ. avaṉai valam varupavaḷ pōl piṉpuṟamāka vantu, avaṉaik kīḻē taḷḷik koṉṟāḷ.
piṉṉar avaḷ ulakai veṟuttuc camaṇa matat tiṟcērntu tuṟavu pūṇṭāḷ. tuṟaviyāṉavuṭaṉ talai mayir kaḷaiyap paṭṭatu. āyiṉum mīṇṭum talaimayir muḷaittuc curuṇṭu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ātalāl ivaḷukkuk kuṇṭalakēci eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kuṇṭalakēci yeṉṟāl curuṇṭa mayiriṉai yuṭaiyavaḷ eṉpatu poruḷ.
ituvē nīlakēciyiṟ kāṇum kataiyākum. ikkataiyiṉai, in nūliṉ katai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatu. āyiṉum, mutaliliruntu iṟutivaraiyiṟ pala māṟutalkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. itaṉ kataic curukkattaiyum kīḻē tarukiṟōm.
tañcai nakar aracaṉ makaḷ kōmaḷavalli. avaḷē paṭṭattiḷavaraci tamiḻk kalvi niṟaintavaḷ. cuntaranātaṉ eṉṉum tiruṭaṉaik kaṇṭāḷ; avaṉ mēl kātal koṇṭāḷ. tiruṭaṉai aracaṉ vicārittuk kolait taṇṭaṉai tantāṉ; kōmaḷavalli avaṉaiyē maṇakkap piṭivātam ceytāḷ; aracaṉ avaṉuṭaiya taṇṭaṉaiyai māṟṟiṉāṉ. avaṉaic cēṉait talaivaṉ ākkiṉāṉ.
maturai maṉṉaṉ piratāpaṉ. avaṉ kōmaḷavalliyiṉ poruṭṭu, tañcai nakar mītu paṭaiyeṭuttu vantāṉ. cuntaranātaṉ avaṉai etirttut tōlviyuṟac ceytāṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya veṟṟikkup paricāka kēmaḷavalliyai maṇam ceytu koṭuttāṉ maṉṉaṉ.
kaḷvaṉum iḷavaraciyum orunāḷ tōṭṭattil kātal moḻipēcik kaḷittiruntaṉar. iruvarum taṅkaḷ kātal veḷip paṭap pāṭikkoṇṭintaṉar, kōmaḷavalli, taṉ kātal mikutiyāl, nī iṉṉum  uṉ tiruṭṭaiyum, kolaiyaiyum maṟantuviṭavillai eṉṟu pāṭiṉāḷ, kaḷvaṉ kaṭuñ ciṉamuṟṟu, atai maṟaittuk koṇṭu, poyyaṉpu kāṭṭi vāḻntu vantāṉ.
kōṭai nāḷ vantatu. veyyiliṉ koṭumaiyāl kōmaḷavalli tuṉpuṟuvatu kaṇṭu, paḻitīrkka atuvē paruvam eṉṟu tiruṭaṉ karutiṉāṉ. avaḷuṭaṉ nīla malaikkuc ceṉṟu taṅkiṉāṉ
orunāṉ maṟaimati nāḷil iruvarum oru malaic cikarattai aṭaintu uraiyāṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar. poḻutu pōyiṟṟu. kōmaḷavalli kaḷvaṉai uṟaiviṭattiṟku aḻait tāḷ. avaṉ taṉ uḷḷattai veḷiyiṭṭu, uṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvattai vaṇaṅkik koḷ eṉṟāṉ. avaḷ nīyē vaḻipaṭu teyvam eṉṟu valam vantāḷ. kālam pārttu avaṉaip paṭukuḻiyil taḷḷiṉāḷ - kaḷvaṉ māṇṭāṉ.
piṉṉar iḷavaraci ūr vantu cērntāḷ. aracaṉ vayatu mutirnta kāraṇattāl aracāṭciyiliruntu vilaka muṭivu ceytāṉ. yāriṭam aracai oppuvippatu eṉṟu avai yiṉarai viṉava, kōmaḷavalliyē aracukkuriyavaḷ eṉṟaṉar.
kōmaḷavalli aracēṟka maṟuttāḷ. kuṭi aracē ciṟanta teṉṟu avaiyiṉarukkuk kūṟiṉāḷ. aṉaivarum oppiṉar aracaṉ vilakiṉāṉ. kuṭikaḷuḷ oruvaṉāṉāṉ kōmaḷavalliyum kuṭikaḷuḷ oruttiyāṉāḷ aracu kuṭiyaracāyiṟṟu.
ituvē aṇainta viḷakkiṉ kataic curukkam. itaṉaiyum, kuṇṭalakēciyiṉ kataiyiṉaiyum oppu nōkkiṉ, vēṟupāṭu viḷaṅkum.
ikkatai innūlil oṉpatu pirivākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. opvoru piriviṉ ciṟanta karuttai viḷakkum aṭikaḷil uḷḷa coṟkaḷaiyē appirivukaḷukkut talaip peyarāka  amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kataippakutic curukkam eṉṉum talaippiṉ kīḻ, ovvoru pakutiyiṉ kataiyaiyum curukkamāka eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. ataip paṭitta piṉ nūlaip paṭittāl, uyttuṇarviṉṟik kataiyai aṟintu paṭikkalām.
peyarkkāraṇam
aṇainta viḷakku innūlukku poruttamāṉa peyarāka irukkumeṉṟu karutiyē appeyar koṭuttēṉ.
veṇpāviṉ, neṭu veṇpāṭṭu, allatu paḵṟoṭai veṇpāvē nāṉkiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa aṭikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. tol kāppiyattil, neṭu veṇpāṭṭukku 12 aṭikaḷ ellai kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
neṭu veṇpāṭṭē munnālaṭittē eṉpatu tol kāppiyam. (tol, cey. 470) piṟkāla nūlākiya yāpparuṅkalak kārikaiyil, neṭu veṇ pāṭṭaiyē paḵṟoṭai veṇpā veṉak kūṟiṉar. piṟkāla ilakkaṇa nūlār palarum ip peyaraiyē kūṟiṉar.
kārikaiyil paḵṟoṭai veṇpāvukku, aṭi varai yaṟai kūṟap paṭavillai. pala aṭikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu paḵṟoṭai veṇpā veṉṟē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
aṭi palavāyc, ceṉṟu nikaḻva paḵṟoṭaiyām eṉpatu kārikai.
tolkāppiyattil aṭivaraiyaṟai kūṟiya pāvukkup piṟkālattār aṭivaraiyaṟai kūṟāmal viṭṭa kāraṇam, putiyaṉa pukutal ākumeṉṟē karutu kiṉṟaṉar cilar. ivvāṟu aṭi palavuṭaiya veṇpāvukku ilakkiyam kiṭaikkavillai. ātaliṉ itaṉai veṇpā vakaiyiṟ cērttal poruntātu. veṇpāttaḷai viraviya maṟṟōr pāṭṭu kali veṇpāvākum. kaliveṇpāvait tolkāppiyar kaliveṇpāṭṭeṉak kuṟippar.
kaliveṇpāṭṭu eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭum ilakkaṇam :-
oru poruḷ nutaliya veḷḷaṭi iyalāṟ
ṟiripiṉṟi varuvatu kaliveṇpāṭṭē
eṉpatu.
cilappatikārattil uḷḷa kaṉāttiṟam uraitta kātai yeṉpatu, ittakaiya veṇpāttaḷai peṟṟa pāṭṭāl ākiyatu. ak kātaiyiṉ iṟutiyil aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiya uraiyil appāṭalaik kaliveṇpāṭṭu eṉṟē kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
itu kaṉaveṉṉum poruḷaiyē karutātu. mālati kataiyaiyum, tēvanti kataiyaiyum kalantu kūṟiṟṟēṉum, uṟuppaḻiviṉṟi naṭantatēṉum, mun nāṉkaṭiyiṉiṟantu varutalāṉ neṭuveṇ pāṭṭākātu kaliveṇpāṭṭāta luṇarka!
eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallār urai ātalāl nām veṇpāttaḷai piṟaḻāmal varukiṉṟa 12 aṭikaḷukku mēṟ paṭṭa pāṭalai kaliveṇpāṭṭu eṉṟē aḻaippōm.
ātalāl in nūliṉ perumpālāṉa pāṭṭukkaḷ kaliveṇ pāṭṭukkaḷ ākum. iṉṉum, viruttappā, āciriyappā, putiya vakaiyil uḷḷa cila pāṭalkaḷum itil kalantuḷḷaṉa.
eḷiya naṭaiyum, iṉiya cantamum, putiya poruḷum uṭaiya pākkaḷ pala tōṉṟavēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ karuttu. aṟiñarkaḷum, itu tamiḻaṉṉaikkup putup paṇi eṉṟu karutu kiṉṟaṉar. in nūlum, in nūṟ pāṭalkaḷum, in nūṟ karuttum, centamiḻaṉṉaikku oru putup pūṇāka irukkalākātā? eṉṟa āvaluṭaṉēyē yāṉum innūlai eḻutiṉēṉ. innūl ciṟitaḷavāvatu tamiḻ vaḷarccikkut tuṇaipuriyumāyiṉ. atuvē eṉ karuttukkuc ciṟanta paricu kiṭaittatākak koṇṭu makiḻvēṉ.
aṉpaṉ,
cāmi.citampaṉār.


cāmi. citapparaṉāriṉ aṇainta
viḷakku

kataip pakutic curukkam
1
cuntaranātaṉ oru peruṅ kaḷvaṉ; avaṉ aṭikkaṭi tañcai nakaraik koḷḷaiyiṭṭu vantāṉ.
tañcai nakar maṉṉaṉ tayāniti. avaṉ maṉaivi taṇ kōtai tēvi. avarkaḷukku orē peṇ. avaḷ peyar kōmaḷavalli. aḻakiṟ ciṟantavaḷ; tamiḻk kalviyiṟ tērntavaḷ. avaḷē paṭṭattiḷavaraci.
kaḷvaṉaip piṭikkak kāvalarkaḷ pala nāṭkaḷ muyaṉṟaṉar. iṟutiyil piṭipaṭṭāṉ. tañcai nakarppuṟattil oru periya ēri. ataṉ karaiyil oru tāḻaṅkāṭu. atil oḷintiruntāṉ cuntaranātaṉ. kāvalar talaivarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ iruḷappaṉ. ivaṉ kaḷvaṉaip piṭittāṉ. kāvalarkaḷ kaḷvaṉukku vilaṅ kiṭṭaṉar. tañcai nakariṉ terukkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē iḻuttu vantaṉar. potumakkaḷ ak kāṭciyaik kaṇṭaṉar. kaḷvaṉaip paṟṟip palap pala pakarntaṉar,. avaṉuṭaiya tōḻarkaḷ palar avaṉai mīṭka vaḻiyiṉṟi varuntic ceṉṟaṉar.
araṇmaṉai māṭiyil mēl iḷavaraci, tōḻikaḷuṭaṉ viḷaiyāṭik koṇṭiruntāḷ. apapoḻutu, kaḷvaṉai, araṇmaṉai vīti vaḻiyāka aḻaittu vantaṉar. iḷavaraci teruvil eḻunta muḻakkattaik kēṭṭāḷ. viyaiṭṭai viṭṭu ōṭi vantu teruvai nōkkiṉāḷ. kaṭṭaḻakuṭaiya cuntaranātaṉaik kaṇṭāḷ; kātal koṇṭāḷ; kaḷvaṉum aṇṇāntu pārttāṉ; aracakumāriyaik kaṇṭāṉ; iruvar kaṇkaḷum oṉṟupaṭṭaṉa.
kāvalar talaivaṉ, aracaṉ muṉ kaḷvaṉaik koṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. aracaṉ iṉṟu iravu ciṟaiyil vaittiruppīr. nāḷai namatu capaikkuk koṇṭu varuka  eṉṟu paṇittāṉ. kaḷvaṉaip piṭitta iruḷapṉukkup paricu pala vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. kaḷvaṉai aṉṟiravu ciṟaiyil vaittaṉar; kāvalar kāttu niṉṟaṉar.
2
ciṟaikkuḷḷirunta cuntaranātaṉukku uṟakkam varavillai. araca kumāriyiṉ tōṟṟam avaṉ neñcai viṭṭu akala villai; avaḷ uruveḷit tōṟṟaṅ kaṇṭu uḷḷaṅ kuḻain tāṉ; uṇmaiyāka avaḷē vantu viṭṭatāka eṇṇiṉāṉ; eḻuntāṉ; naṭantāṉ; āṭiṉāṉ; pāṭiṉāṉ; pataṟiṉāṉ. kāvalar avaṉaip pittaṉeṉṟu ikaḻntaṉar. kaḷvaṉuṭaiya kaṭuneñcam kārikaiyiṉ nōkkāl kaṉivaṭaintatu.
araca kumāriyum, araṇmaṉaiyil kātalāl kalaṅki niṉṟāḷ. kaḷvaṉuruvaik karuttiṟ patittāḷ  avaṉai maṇappa tevvāṟu eṉṟu karutic cōrntāḷ. tōḻiyar avaḷ nōy kaṇṭu varunti niṉṟaṉar. avaḷ nōyai nīkkap palavita paṇi viṭaikaḷ ceytu niṉṟaṉar.
3
poḻutum pularntatu. kaḷvaṉ vaḻakkaip pārkkum poruṭṭu ūrār tiraṇṭaṉar; aracaṉuṭaiya avaik kaḷattai aṭaintaṉar. amaiccarkaḷum aracavai vantu amarntaṉar.
tayāniti aṉṟu naṭaipeṟap pōkum, kaḷvaṉ vaḻakkaip paṟṟik kōmaḷavallikku viḷampiṉāṉ. avaḷaiyum aḻaittuk koṇṭu capaikku vantāṉ; ariyācaṉattil amarntāṉ. iḷavaraciyum, oru puṟattil uṭkārntāḷ.
kāvalarkaḷ kaḷvaṉaik koṇṭu vantu aracaṉmuṉ niṟuttiṉar. aracaṉ kaḷvaṉuṭaiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉāṉ. kaḷvaṉ aracaṉai etirttu, nīyum eṉ pōṉṟa tiruṭaṉ; kolaikāraṉ; nammiruvaḷ vēṟṟumaiyillai eṉṟāṉ. aracaṉ cīṟṟaṅ koṇṭu avaṉai veṭṭutaṟku vāḷeṭuttāṉ.
āymatiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ mutal amaiccaṉ. avaṉ virain teḻuntu maṉṉaṉ kaiyaip piṭittuk koṇṭāṉ. tiruṭaṉ moḻiyiṉ karuttai naṉṟāka uṇarntu koḷḷu vōm; piṟaku taṇṭaṉai koṭuppōm eṉṟāṉ. maṉṉaṉum ciṉantaṇintu uṭkārntāṉ.
āymatiyaṉ kaḷvaṉai nōkki. aracaṉum uṉ pōṉṟavaṉ eṉpatu evvāṟu? viḷaṅkac col eṉṟāṉ.
kaḷvaṉ nāṉ tiruṭukiṉṟēṉ; eṉṉaip piṭikka varuvōraik kollukiṉṟēṉ. celvar poruḷaiyē koḷḷai koḷvēṉ; taṟkāppukkākavē kolai ceyvēṉ. aracaṉ uḻaippāḷar poruḷai variyiṉ vaḻiyākak koḷḷai koḷḷu kiṉṟāṉ;pōr purivataṉ mūlam kolai ceykiṉṟāṉ. itu kolaiyaṉṟō? eṉṟāṉ.
amaiccaṉ aracaṉ ceyal, nāṭṭu nalaṅ karutiyatu; āṉṟōrāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu; nūlātaravuḷḷatu; veḷippaṭaiyākac ceyyappaṭuvatu. uṉ ceyal ellōrālum ikaḻappaṭuvatu eṉṟāṉ. kaḷvaṉ itaṟku maṟumoḻi kūṟāmal niṉṟāṉ.
aracaṉ, kaḷvaṉukkut taṇṭaṉai yātu? eṉṟu avai yiṉaraik kēṭṭāṉ. kolait taṇṭaṉaiyē takkatu eṉaṟu ellērum kūṟiṉar.
veṟṟinātaṉ eṉṉum amaiccaṉ ivaṉ iḷaiñaṉ añcā neñcaṉ; pakuttaṟivāḷaṉ; ātalāṟ kolla vēṇṭām. ivaṉaic cīrtirutti nallōṉākki nāṭṭut toṇṭukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām eṉṟāṉ. avaiyiṉar ivvamaiccaṉ moḻiyai yikaḻntaṉar.aracaṉum avamatittāṉ  kaḷvaṉukku maraṇa taṇṭaṉai eṉṟu muṭivu kūṟiṉāṉ maṉṉaṉ. 
maraṇa taṇṭaṉai eṉṟa muṭivu kēṭṭatum kaḷvaṉ tikaittāṉ; iḷavaraci tiṭukkiṭṭāḷ; avaiyōr makiḻntaṉar. iḷavaraciyiṉ kaṇkaḷum, kaḷvaṉ kaṇkaḷum cankittaṉa. kaḷvaṉ cōrvuṟṟuk kīḻē viḻuntāṉ. iḷavaraciyum pēccu mūccaṟṟāḷ; cōrntāḷ; cāyntu viṭṭāḷ. avaiyōr ikkāṭci kaṇṭaṉar; viyantaṉar iḷavaraciyai araṇmaṉaikku eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉar. avai kalaintatu.
4
kaḷvaṉ peṟṟa kaṭuntaṇṭaṉaiyāl iḷavaraci āvi tuṭittāḷ; cōrntu kiṭantāḷ. aṉṟu pakalum, iravum aracaṉum, araciyum tōḻiyarum avaḷ pakkattiliruntu  varuntiṉar; tuyaram taṇiyap paṇi viṭaikaḷ pala purintaṉar; maruttuvarkaḷ palar maruntaḷittaṉar. avaḷ tuṉpam māṟa villai.
maṟu nāḷ poḻutu viṭintatu. aṉṟutāṉ kaḷvaṉaik kolai ceyyum nāḷ. iḷavaraci, tāṉ kaḷvaṉ mītu koṇṭirukkum kātalai mella veḷiyiṭṭāḷ. atu kēṭṭa aṉaivarum tikaittaṉar. aracaṉ nī tiruṭaṉ mītu vaitta ācai kātal alla; kāmamē; atai maṟantu viṭu eṉṟāṉ taṇkōtai tēviyum evvaḷavō kūṟiṉaḷ. kōmaḷavalli  kātal vēṟaṉṟu; kāmam vēṟaṉṟu; iraṇṭum oṉṟē; kaṭṭaḻakaṉai maṇakkāviṭil uyir vāḻēṉ eṉṟu muṭivākak kūṟiviṭṭāḷ. taṇkōtai tēviyum, kaḷvaṉuṭaiya kolait taṇṭaṉaiyai māṟṟumāṟu aracaṉai vēṇṭiṉaḷ.
aracaṉum maṉamiraṅkiṉāṉ. cuntaranātaṉuṭaiya kolait taṇṭaṉaiyai māṟṟi viṭṭatāka oru ōlai yeḻutiṉāṉ. atai oru tōḻiyiṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttāṉ; kolaik kaḷattil uḷḷa atikāriyiṉ kaiyiṟ cērkkum paṭi paṇittāṉ. tōḻiyum, avvōlaiyai vāṅkik koṇṭu virain tōṭiṉāḷ.
5
nalla naṭuppakal. kolaik kaḷattil kaḷvaṉ oru mēṭaiyiṉ mēl niṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ. pala makkaḷ kuḻumiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. cilar avaṉ māḷap pōvatu karuti iraṅkukiṉṟaṉar, palar avaṉ māḷap pōvatu karuti makiḻcciyaṭaikiṉṟaṉar. ic camayattil, kolait taṇṭaṉaiyai niṟaivēṟṟum atikāri tiruṭaṉ talaiyait tuṇikku māṟu kolaiyāḷikaḷukku aṟivittāṉ. oru kolaiñaṉ kaḷvaṉ kaḻuttiṟku nēraka vāḷai ōṅkiṉāṉ.āṉāl veṭṭu viḻumāṟu vīcavillai.
iccamayattil kollātē! nillu! kolait taṇṭaṇaiyai māṟṟiṉāṉ maṉṉavaṉ eṉṟu kūvik koṇṭu ōṭi vantāḷ oru peṇ. avaḷ aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyai atikāri yiṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttāḷ. atikāri kolait taṇṭaṉaiyai niṟuttiṉāṉ.kaḷvaṉai aracavaikkuk koṇṭu pōyiṉar. itu kaṇṭu potu makkaḷ viyappuṟṟup paṟpala moḻintaṉar.
aracavai kūṭiyiruntatu. palarum cūḻntiruntaṉar. aracaṉ  nēṟṟu veṟṟinātaṉ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ eṉ maṉattiṟ patintaṉa. ātalāl kolait taṇṭaṉaiyai māṟṟiṉēṉ; ik kaṭṭiḷaiñaṉaic cīrtiruttalāmeṉṟu karutukiṉṟēṉ. uṅkaḷ eṇṇam yātu? uraimiṉ eṉṟāṉ veṟṟinātaṉ maṉṉaṉ ceykaiyai ātarittāṉ. ivvīraṉai namatu cēṉait talaivaṉākkutal nalam  eṉṟāṉ.
āymatiyaṉ ivaṉ nal vaḻiyil naṭappatāka uṟuti kūṟiṉāl avvāṟē ceyyalām eṉṟāṉ.
maṉṉaṉ kaḷvaṉai nōkki, uṉ eṇṇam eṉṉa veṉṟāṉ. kaḷvaṉ nāṉ vaṟumaiyāl koṭumai cey tēṉ. iṉi oḻuṅkāka naṭappēṉ. eṉakkuk koṭukkum vēlaiyait tiṟampaṭac ceyvēṉ eṉṟāṉ.
aracaṉ appōtē kaḷvaṉaic cēṉait talaivaṉāk kiṉāṉ; varicai pala tantāṉ. immuṭivaic cilar veṟuttaṉar; cilar pukaḻntaṉar. avai kalaintapiṉ aracaṉ iḷavaraciyiṭam ceṉṟāṉ. innikaḻccikaḷai aṟivittāṉ; avaḷ tuṉpam nīṅki yiṉpa muṟṟāḷ.
6
maturai nakar maṉṉaṉ piratāpaṉ; maṇamākātavaṉ. avaṉukkup palar peṇ koṭukka muṉvantatum, maṇam puriya maṟuttiruntāṉ. avaṉ oru nāḷ taṉiyāka araṇmaṉait tōṭṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟāṉ. aṅkē oru mēṭaiyil uṭkārn tāṉ. pakkattu marakkiḷaiyil iru kuyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. avai kātal moḻi pecik kaḷittaṉa. atu kaṇṭa maṉṉaṉ oru puttuṇarcci peṟṟāṉ aṉṟu mutal avaṉ maṉatil kavalai kuṭi koṇṭatu. ak kavalai mukattil veḷippaṭṭiruntatu.
amaiccarkaḷ avaṉuṭaiya mukac cōrvu kaṇṭaṉar. avaṉukku maṇamuṭippatē avaṉ kavalai tīrvataṟku vaḻi yeṉṟu muṭivu kaṇṭaṉar. aracaṉai yaṭaintaṉar. nīṅkaḷaṉai varum vantatu eṉ karuti? uraimiṉ! eṉṟāṉ maṉṉaṉ. mutalaimaiccaṉ cukuṇaṉ, kōmaḷa valliyiṉ aḻakaiyum, aṟivaiyum eṭuttuk kūṟi. avaḷē uṉakkēṟṟa peṇ avaḷai maṇantāl nī makiḻcciyaṭaivāy eṉṟāṉ maṉṉaṉum maṇam purintukeḷḷa oppiṉāṉ.
piratāpaṉukkup peṇ koṭukkumāṟu tayānitikku amaiccarkaḷ kaṭitam viṭuttaṉar. pala nāṭkaḷ kaṭanta piṉ, tayānitiyiṉuṭaiya maṟumoḻi kiṭaittatu; piratāpaṉukkup peṇ koṭukka maṟuttāṉ. uṭaṉē piratāpaṉ ciṉaṅ koṇṭāṉ.
amaiccarkaḷ cūḻcciyiṉpaṭi, piratāpaṉ paṭai tiraṭṭic ceṉṟu tañcai nakarai muṟṟukai yiṭṭāṉ.
iccamayam, tayāniti araca maṉṟattiliruntāṉ amaiccar kaḷum. piṟarum cūḻntiruntaṉar. oṟṟarkaḷ ōṭivantu piratāpaṉ paṭaiyeṭuttu vantirukkum ceytiyaik kūṟiṉar. atu kēṭṭu aracaṉ tikaittāṉ; amaiccarkaḷ viḻittaṉar.
cuntaranātaṉ eḻuntāṉ. nāṉ ceṉṟu piratāpaṉaiyum, avaṉuṭaiya paṭaikaḷaiyum muṟiyaṭittu varukiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ. aracaṉum makiḻntu viṭai tantāṉ. cuntaranātaṉ, taṉ cēṉai kaḷuṭaṉ nakarppuṟattai yaṭaintāṉ piratāpaṉ paṭaiyuṭaṉ pōr toṭuttāṉ piratāpaṉum, avaṉ paṭai vīrarkaḷum tōṟṟup puṟamutukiṭṭaṉar.
cuntaranātaṉiṉ veṟṟiyaik kēṭṭu maṉṉavaṉum makkaḷum mikiḻntaṉar. maṉṉavaṉ  cuntaranākaṉ peṟṟa veṟṟikkup paricāka kōmaḷavalliyai maṇañ ceytu  koṭukkap pōkiṟēṉ. avaḷum ivaṉ mēl ārāta kātal koṇṭāḷ eṉṟāṉ palarum naṉṟē yeṉṟaṉar; cilar iḵteṉṉa viparītam! eṉṟu veṟuttaṉar.
tirumaṇattukku nāḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. tamiḻ muṟaippaṭi cuntara nātaṉukkum iḷavaracikkum kaṭimaṇam nikaḻntatu maṉṉaṉ makiḻntu paricu pala vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ makkaḷum makiḻntaṉar. kaḷvaṉum iḷavaraciyum kalantu makiḻntu vāḻntu vantaṉar.
7
araṇmaṉaic cōlai. atil oru tāmarai pūtta taṭākam; ataṉ karaiyil oru maṇṭapam; ammaṇṭapattil ōrmēṭai; am mēṭaiyil cuntaranāṉum iḷavaraciyum vīṟṟiruntaṉar. kātalmoḻi pala pēcik kaḷittaṉar kāviṉ vaḷaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭumakiḻntaṉar. oruvarai oravar pārāṭṭip pukaḻntaṉar. cuntaranātaṉai oru pāṭal pāṭavēṇṭiṉaḷ. kaḷvaṉ iḷavaraciyiṉ kātalē taṉṉai nalvaḻippaṭuttiṟṟu eṉṉum karuttup paṭap pāṭiṉāṉ. piṟaku avaḷum taṉ kātalai veḷiyiṭṭup pāṭiṉāḷ avaḷ taṉatu pāṭṭil nī eṉṉai uṉmaṉattil tiruṭi vaittirukkiṉṟāy! nī ciṟitu nēram eṉṉai viṭṭup pirintiruntālum, appirivu eṉṉaik kollukiṟatu ātalāl uṉ paḻantoḻilai iṉṉum nī viṭavillai eṉṟa karuttai veḷiyiṭṭāḷ. tiruṭaṉ avaḷ kātaṟ karuttai uṇara muṭiyāmal ciṉamuṟṟāṉ. avaṉ taṉ ciṉattai veḷiyiṭāmal maṟaittuk koṇṭāṉ. piṉṉum ciṟitu nēram avarkaḷ aṅkē yiruntuviṭṭup poḻutu paṭṭatum araṇmaṉaikkup pōyiṉar.
8
aṉṟu mutal kaḷvaṉ kalakkamuṟṟāṉ. avaṉuṭaiya paḻaya ceyalkaḷaip paṟṟiya niṉaivukaḷ vantuviṭṭaṉa. piṟar taṉṉai nōkkum pōtellām. kaḷvaṉeṉṟu karuti nōkkuvatākavē niṉaittāṉ piṟar pārkkum pōtellām nāṇit talai kaviḻntāṉ. avaṉukku araca vāḻvu puḷittuviṭṭatu; kōmaḷa valliyiṉ mēl veṟuppuṟṟāṉ; avaḷaip paḻivāṅkutaṟkuk kālaṅkarutiniṉṟāṉ.
kōṭai nāḷ piṟantatu. veyyiṟ koṭumai tāṅka muṭiyavillai veppattiṉ mikutiyāl iḷavaraciyiṉ uṭampil koppuḷaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa ituvē avaḷai paḻivāṅkac camayam eṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. cuntaranātaṉ iḷavaraciyai nōkki kōṭaik koṭumai nīṅka, nīlakirikkuc celvōm eṉṟāṉ avaḷum uṭaṉpaṭṭāḷ iruvarum pōtiya ēvalar kaḷuṭaṉ nīlamalaiyai aṭaintaṉar. aṅkōr māḷikaiyil taṅkiṉar.
palanāṭkaḷ nīlakiriyil vāḻntaṉar. oru amāvācaināḷ aṉṟu mālai cuntaranātaṉum iḷavariciyum oru kuvaṭṭai yaṭaintaṉar. kīḻē kiṭukiṭu paḷḷam. avviṭattil uṭ kārntu iruvarum kātal moḻi pēciṉar iruḷ cūḻntatu atu kaṇṭu iḷavaraci nēramāyiṟṟu viṭutikkuc celvōm eṉṟāḷ cuntaranātaṉ nī eṉṉaik kaḷvaṉ kolaikāraṉ eṉṟu cerukkāl ikaḻntāy! ātalāl uṉṉaik koṉṟu eṉ veñciṉattait tīrttukkoḷḷap pōkiṟēṉ. ippoḻutē uṉatu vaḻipaṭu teyvattai vaṇaṅkikkoḷ eṉṟāṉ.
iḷavaraci, eṉṉaik kolla niṉaikkum iv vañca kaṉai nāṉ kolvēṉ  eṉṟu muṭivu ceytu koṇṭāḷ. kaḷvaṉai nōkki,nīyē eṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvam! uṉṉai yaṉṟi vēṟu teyvamaṟiyēṉ! eḻuntu nil; valam vantu vaṇaṅku kiṟēṉ eṉṟāḷ. tiruṭaṉum eḻuntu niṉṟāṉ. avaḷ avaṉaic cuṟṟi varumpōtu, takka camayam pārttup paṭukuḻiyil taḷḷi viṭṭāḷ avaṉum āveṉṟalaṟi vīḻntu māṇṭāṉ.
itu nikaḻnta piṉ iḷavaraci tāṅkā-t tuyaruṟṟāḷ vāy viṭṭu araṟṟiṉāḷ. araṟṟik koṇṭē, avviṭattai viṭṭu iṟaṅki vantu koṇṭiruntāḷ. itankuḷ ēvalarkaḷ avaḷai yum cuntaranātaṉaiyum tēṭi vantaṉar. avarkaḷ. iḷavaraciyiṉ aḻukural kēṭṭu avaḷai yaṭaintu, aḻaittuc ceṉṟaṉar. naṭanta nikaḻcaciyai ēvalarkaḷum aṟintu tuṉpuṟṟaṉar. aṉai varum aṉṟiravu naṭantu tañcai yaṭaintaṉar. iḷavaraciyiṉ ceyti tañcainakar muḻuvatum paraviṭṭatu.
9
tañcai nakar muḻuvatum iḷavaraciyai paṟṟiyē pēccu; aracaṉaip paṟṟiyum, kaḷvaṉaip paṟṟiyum palar pēciṉar aracaṉum, araciyum, iḷavaraciyiṉ nilaikuṟittu varuntiṉar vayatu mutirnta maṉṉaṉ āṭciyil veṟuppuṟṟāṉ; araca vaiyaik kūṭṭiṉāṉ. avaiyiṉarai nōkkināṉ taḷḷāta paruvamuṟṟēṉ. kōmaḷavalli kaḷvaṉai maṇantu kaṭum paḻikku āḷāyiṉaḷ. iṉi aracāḷuvatu yār? uṅkaḷ viruppam eṉṉa? eṉṟāṉ.
mutal mantiri kōmaḷavalliyē aracāḷalām avaḷ kaḷvaṉaik koṉṟatu vīrac ceyalē ataṉāl paḻiyoṉṟu millai. eṉṟāṉ ellōrum amaiccaṉ moḻiyai ātarittaṉar.
maṉṉaṉ kōmaḷavalliyiṉ mukattai nōkkiṉāṉ. avaḷ nāṉ kātalai nampik keṭṭēṉ. ulaivāykku mūṭiyuṇṭu; ūr vāykku mūṭiyillai aḻiyāp paḻipūṇṭa nāṉ araciyāka virumpavillai. kuṭikaḷē tāṅkaḷ virumpum oruvarait tērnteṭuttu āṭci puriyalām. kuṭi aracu ciṟanta aracu eṉṟāḷ. 
avaḷ moḻiyai aracaṉum amaiccarkaḷum, potu makkaḷum oppuk koṇṭaṉar.
uṭaṉē aracaṉ, nīṅkaḷē aracāḷuṅkaḷ! nāṉ uṅkaḷuḷ oruvaṉāyiruntu aracāṭcikku utavi ceykiṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟa, kōmaḷa valliyum iṉṟumutal nāṉum kuṭikaḷuḷ oruttiyāy iruppēṉ. tamiḻ moḻikkum tamiḻ makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum uḻaippēṉ. aracukkum utavuvēṉ eṉṟāḷ.
makkaḷum, taṅkaḷuṭ cilarai oru kuḻuvākat tērn teṭuttaṉar. akkuḻuviṉaruḷ oruvaṉait talaivaṉākkiṉar kuṭi aracukku vēṇṭiya caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷamaittaṉar. kuṭiyaracāki iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntu vantaṉar.

Foreword by the Authour to
“Anaintha Vilaku”

(Transl ation)
It was my ambition to create a good story in mellodious verse.  aṇainta viḷakku (Blown out lamp) is the out- come of it. This is not my pure imaginary creation. It is a subtle mixture of an ancient story and mine own stuff. The basis of this story is adapted from Kundalakesi, an epic.
Kundalakesi is one among the five celebrated great Tamil epic Poems, the four other being, Silapathikaram, Chintamani, Manimekalai and Valayapathi. The Story proper of Kundalakesi, as narrated in that great epic is not at available anywhere in the world, as the Poem is a lost treasure. Still, the outline and synopsis of the story are said to contain in Theri Kaathai, Dhamma Patha and Angutharaniyaka which are of Pali literature. Besides, in the commentary of verse 286 in Nilakesi, a Jainist Philosophy, the following summary of Kundalakesi is manifested.
The Prime-minister of the kingdom of Raja Graham had a beautiful grown-up daughter named Baddhirai. One day as The was strolling on the balcony of her mansion she beheld a handsome youth led away by gaolers to the gallows. Instantly she fell in love with him. That unhappy fellow who was the target of this “Love at first sight” had been the son of the king’s Priest. the Criminal was condemned to death for High way Robbery.
As Badhirai expressed her desire to marry this condemned man above as her spouse. and admently stated that she would never marry anyone else, the minister greased the palms of the gaolers and saved the dacoit from the halter.
Later the prime - minister gave Baddhirai in marriage to that thief. They lived happily for many days. But as illuck would have it, one night in a lighter vein she remarked that after all he was a Robber! Though he pretended to have swallowed the insult, he never forgot or forgave that humiliation.
Later, one day he invited his wife to accompany him to a hillock, where he was proceeding, he claimed, to offer his grateful and thank-giving prayers to the deity that saved him from death. As she readily consented and went with him to the top of the hillock he shouted at her and cried, “Now fall in worship to any deity that you hold sacred, as you are going to meet with your end at my own hands in a few minutes. “How adacious had you been to call me a Robber at my own face!” She was much too shocked at this unexpected outburst. However, wise, clever and intelligent that she was, she decided to outwit him. “Whoever be a greater god than a husband to a chaste wife?” asked she and cajoled him, “Pray, permit me to circumambulate you, my Lord, and offer my humble prayers at your feet” As he nodded in consenm, she went round him, and kicked him down into the steep valley to his death. Thus the rogue met with the poetic justice.
As Baddirai felt disgusted with the way of this mundane world, she embraced jainism, became a Sanyasini, and had her head completely tonsured according to Jain tradition and rites. But lo! Her hairlock again grew lustily in all magestic waves and curls. She was hence called “Kundalakesi”, a term which denotes curly hairlocks.
This is the story which Nilakesi offers us. Our story in this book is broadly based upon it. But there is a vast difference throughout. Synopsis of our story that follows is as here under: 
the crown princess  Komalavalli is the only issue of the king of Thanjavur, She is a scholer in Tamil. She met with Sundaranathan, a thief, and fell in love with him. As the king condenned him to death for his crime, the princess intervened to express her desire that she would ever remain a spinster if he is executed, as she had already given room in her bosom as her only husband. The king remmitted the sentence, and made him the chief of the Army.
Prathapan, the king of Madurai, in his lust towards Komalavalli invaded on Thanjavur, sundaranathan met him on the battle-field and won his victory. The king, immensely pleased at this valour of his army- chief, gave his daughter in marriage to him.
One day as the thief and the princess were mutually adoring each other in amorous speech and verse, Komalavalli expressed that he has not yet given up robbery nor murders. He was highly incensed. But he never showed his mind out.
As summer came, Komalavalli was found suffering from the agonies of summer - heat. He thought it was the proper moment for him to avenge himself. He took her to some hill resort.
On a moonless night as the couple were spending happy moments on the top of a hill, the robber burst out his anger and ordered her to offer her last prayers to her deity. She went round him with folded hands calling him as the only deity and at the right moment pushed him down the precipice to his doom.
Later as she arrived home, her old father due to advance in age offered to abdicate his throne in favour of his daughter. But she refused the crown. she favoured only the Republican form of government, which was readily accepted by all. the king after his abdication had himself become an ordinary citizen, so also his daughter, and the kingdom became a Republic.
This is the summary of the story of this book. A comparison would reveal the difference between this story and that of Kundalakesi.
This story is divided into nine parts in this book. This heading of each part is named in the every words that are employed in the verses of the climax of such parts. Under the heading of Synopses of story proper in each part I have given the outlines of the narration. If the book is read in the footsteps of such synopses, the whole story will reel in easy flow.
The Title
I have chosen the title “Blown out lamp” in earnest and hope this would justify the cause. Corresponding verse to denote this title is found in the ninth part of this book. Komalavalli’s lamentation - “I am indeed a blown out lamp!” is called out from there to adorn the title of this book.
Komalavalli was indeed a  flame of glory in the fields of culture, knowledge, wisdom, and beauty. Her lusture diminished as she married a thief. The flame of her happy days was after all blown out. This is subtly expressed in her lamentation. Hence, the title of this book is shaped from the heroine’s tragie life proper.
The book
Some cheif aims of this book are:
1. 	If there is no good political system of govement, no country, no citizen can ever live in peace. Therefore it is that there are constitutions framed in each land.
2. 	The reason why citizens resort to robbery, larceny. murder etc. is poverty and unemployment. Therefore, it is the duty of the govt. to erase them out.
3. 	It is a mistake to call love ‘divine’, and lust “beast-like” Love is a common phenomenon, whereas lust is a unique feature. The essence of love is lust. Lust, in general, means happiness. Hence, it is not proper to praise one and condemn the other totally.
4.  	 However much a lady is lettered and learned she is disappointed in her love or lust. Men can easily dupe them or cheat them. Women-flolk are seldom equipped with clairvoyance.
5.  	 It is the duty of the government, of a state to convert the criminals into law-abiding citizens.
6.	 Those who are indulged habitually in criminal dealings since their childhood cannot be easily corrected. Bad habits never get erased quickly.
7. 	 Republican form of government is by far better than one-man rule.
These ideas are specially conveyed in this book- Besides, some ideals and imaginary description of natural events also find their place.
Poems
The verses of this book generally follow the pattern set by Post Bharathi in his “Kuyil Paatu” and Bharathi Dasan in his “Sanjeevi Parvatham” Following verse are composed in the metrics of “Venba”
Generally a Venba contains four lines only, but those exceeding four and reaching 12 lines are called “Nedu Ven Paattu” or “Pahrodai Venba” neṭu veṇ pāṭṭē munnālaṭittē (tol, cey, 470) The Tholkappiam sets 12 lines as maximum limit of such lyrics.
Yapparungalak Kaarikai calls “Nedu Ven Paattu” as “Pahrodai Venba” Later grammarians also follow this rule.
The Kaarikai does not prescibe the maximum number of lines of a Pahrodai Venba, though it mentions that such Venbas contain more number of lines or verses: aṭi palavāyc ceṉṟu nikaḻva paḵṟoṭaiyām (kārikai)
Whereas Tholkappiam prescribed the maximum limit, later grammarians conveniently omitted the same to accommodate the later innovations of succeeding generations. Hence it is permissible, they claim. There are no literature available to show Venba of many lines. Therefore, it is not right to include the same in Venba series. But there is a lyric called “Kali Venba” which qualifies the qualities of Venbas. Tholkappiar calls Kali Venba as Kaliven Paattu”.
1.  	 Definition given in Tholkappiam for Kaliven Paattu” is as follows:
oru poruḷ nutaliya veḷḷaṭi iyalāṟ
ṟiripiṉṟi varuvatu kaliveṇ pāṭṭē.
2. There is a sub story under the title kaṉāttiṟam uraitta kātai in Silappathikaram composed in Venba lyrics. Towards the end of that story Adiyarkku Nallar (a commentator) writers of say that poem is “Kaliven Paattu”. He says: - itu kaṉaveṉṉum poruḷaiyē karutātu, mālati kataiyaiyum, tēvanti katai yaiyum kalantu kūṟiṟṟēṉum, uṟuppaḻiviṉṟi naṭantatēṉum mun nāṉkaṭiyi ṉiṟantu varutalāṉ neṭu veṇpāṭṭākātu kali veṇ pāṭṭāta luṇarka.
3. 	 This is Adiyarkku Nallar’s commentary. Hence, we call any stanza containing more than 12 lines, but composed in the metrics of Venba, as a Kali Venba.
Many lyrics and verse of this book are of Kaliven Paattu metrics. Besides, Viruthappa, Aasiriyappa, and modern verses also are employed here.
That easy style, sweet melody and modern meanings should adorn any poem is my dictum. Learned authors also consider such poems as new service to the Mother Tamil. How I hope that this book of mine containing such poems and ideals will be a new jewel in the crest of sweet Tamil. I wrote this only with such a cherised desire. If this book in its own humble way assists the growth of Tamil, I shall be highly elated and delighted as the one who gained the great prize.
Anban
SAMI. CHIDAMBARANAR

STORY OF  “ANAINTHA VILAKU”
By Sami. Chidambaranar
Sundaranathan was a great robber. He was often plundering the city of Tanjore.
Dayanidhi was the king of Tanjore. His wife was Thankothai Devi. They had an only daughter - Komalavalli. She was of great beauty and was well educated in Tamil method of studies. She was the crown princess.
The king’s men took great efforts for many days to catch the theif. At last their efforts fructified. On the outskirts of the city of Tanjore ran a big river, on the banks of which had grown great forest, dark with bushes and trees. There Sundaranathan was hiding himself. At last Irulappan one of the armdty  chiefs of the king traced the thief and arrested him.
The arrested robber was manacled adtnd dragged through the wide streets of the great city of Tanjore. People witnessing the scense spoke a thousand trivial things about the thief. His companions could not liberate him. Unable as they were they returned to the forest with grief.
On the terrace of the palace the princess was playing with her friends. Attracted by the noise of the people she left her play and came running to the street. There she saw the handsome Sundaranathan; he saw her; their eyes met; that one moment of ecstacy! Oh! love had born at first sight.
The army chief brought the thief before the king. The king ordered him to be put behind the bars that night and to be brought before him the next day. Irulappan was given great many rewards. Sundaranathan was locked in the prison; Vigilant soldiers were placed to watch him.
II
Sundaranathan was sleepless in the prison; the beautious form of the princess occupied his mind and eyes; he felt as if she hereself was present there; he sung with all the fanciful and mad delight of a lover; the soldiers laughed at his mad behaviour. His heartlessness left him because he had given his heart to a beautiful graceful damsel whom he loved with all frenzy.
The princess, state in the palace was none the better. She dreamt of the thief. She was disturbed and dissipatted by the thought of her marriage with him may remain a dream for ever. Her friends were all in great sympathy of her state and strived hard to alluviate her grief. But instead it grew.
III
The next day dawned beautifully but it was a painfull beauty to the lovers. Immense throngs were pouring in at the kings court to witness the trial. The king came with his entourage and occudtpied the torone. He was sorrounded by wise ministers.
Komalavalli was also present there because the king had told her about the trial and had taken her to the court.
The thief was produced before the king. The king levelled many charges against him. But the robber unafraid  retorted “Oh king! you are also a thief; murderer. There is no difference between us” he said. The king infuriated took his sword to behead the thief.
But Aaimathiyan. the chief minister of the king stopped the king; and admonished, “Oh king; let us know what the robber implies and then punish him.” His rage alluviated the king settled in his royal seat.
“Oh thief” interrogated Aaimathiyan “how there come no difference between the king and you? tell me”
Replies the robber. “Yes. Your majesty! I commit thievery; and I kill those who try to trap me. Moreover I rob only the rich and I kill only in self defence. But the king, What he does? He robs the toiling peasants and labourers in the name of taxes; he commits homicide in wars. Is it not robbery? Is it not murder?”
The learned counsellor said “But oh thief! the king’s acts are aimed at the country’s welfare. It is accepted by the wise and admired by many a man. It is done in public. But your act is despised by all.
The thief could not answer; he remained silent. Terible wave of silence reigned at the moments that glided away.
At last the king consulted the counsellors as to what punishment is to be given. It was agreed that he should be beheaded.
But vetrinathan, a learned counsellor of the court interfered and said “your majesty! this man is young and brave; moreover he is wise. Kill him not. Reform him and make him usefull to the country’s service”. But the people rediculed the learned words of Vetrinathan. The king also turned deaf ears of his words.
The death Sentence was confirmed.
On the pro nonuncement of the death sentence the robber was bewildered but the courtiers were happy. The eyes of the thief and the princess met. The thief fell down in great grief. The princess was also deeply grieved and she swooned. The courtiers wondered at the happenings. The court was dispersed and the princess was taken to the palace.
IV
The punishment pronounced upon the thief plunged the princess in great sorrow. Her heart throbbed at the thought; She swooned. Everybody in the palace including the king and Queen attended her. They tried their best to heal her ailment but it did not.
The next day’s sun rose above the rough mountains and the vast seas. That day had been fixed for the beheading of the robber. The princess, encouraged by her pure love, exhibidtted it to her parents. They were greatly bewildered. “Your love on the robber is not love” said the king. “But it is mere lust. Forget it” Thankothai Devi also persuaded her. But Komalavalli would not give up. She argued that love and lust are the same and ultimately said that she would not live if she was not married to her hand some lover.
Then the Queen also persuaded the king to change the death sentence.
The king took great pity at the lover of his beautiful daughter. He sent a signed message, altering the death sentence. A girl of the palace hurried to the execution place.
V
It was noon. The scorching sun was at the zenith. The robber was standing on a raised place. A great mob was around him excited. Some people pitied him and others were happy that the robber is going to die. The king’s officer has given the command. The killer raised the sword over the thieves neck.
All of a sudden a cry stopped him. A girl was running towards the killer shouting “Stop! kill him not! The king has altered the sentence!” The message was handed over to the official. The execution was stopped. The robber was taken to the king’s court. The people were astonished by the happenings of the day.
In the court. the king declared. “I am impressed by Vetrinathan’s words. Hence only we stopped the execution. We think it befitting that this young man may be reformed. What do you opine?
Vetrinathan approved of the king’s action and suggested that Sundaranathan may be made the chief of the army.
Aaimathiyan approved of the king’s action and suggested that Sundaranathan may be made the chief of the army.
Aaimathiyan also said that he would accept the same provided the robber promises that he behave in a good manner. Then the king questioned the robber as to his intention.
The robber replied “Your majestry, it was my poverty which compelled me to commit robbery. From now onword I would be a different man; and I would do unto perfection whatever you command”
At once the robber was made the commander of the king’s force. The king  gave him fabulous prizes. Some of the courtiers hated the king’s decision but some others appreciated it.
The king went to the princess and told her about the happenings; her sorrow vanished like snow before the sun.
VI
Madurai - it was an adjacent territory to Tanjore and its king was Prathabhan. Many a king was ready to marry their daughters to him. But he did not assent to many. One fine evening he went to the garden in his palace. He sat on one of the many beautiful benches in the park. In a nearby treebranch were perched two lovebirds. They were wooing. On seeing the love of the birds, a sudden flash of a feeling had come into him. Henceforward he became sorrowful.
The ministers in his court found that the only penance to his newborn sorrow is marying a beautiful girl to him. They approached the king and Sugunan, the Chief minister told him about the beauty and intelligence of Komalavalli. The king accepted to marry her.
Message was sent to Dayanidhi. After waiting for many days they got the reply from Dayanidhi declining the offer.
Prathabhan got enraged. He invaded the city of Tanjore with his forces.
Dayanidhi convened the court immediately. The spies of the king informed him of the unpending invasion. He was astonished. Even the ministers could not utter a syllable.
But Sundaranathan  rose up. “I shall go with my forces and vanquish the proud alien king.” he swore. The king also happily granted permission. The armies faced each other on the battle field in the outskirts of the city. Prathabhan wad defeated and his forces were driven away.
The king and the people were filled with joy on hearing about the great victory. He was given a grand welcome in the court. The king declared “I give my daughter Komalavalli in marriage to Sundaranathan as a trophy of his great victory. She also is in deep love with him.” Many a man appreciated the decision; a few people hated this.
The royal wedding was celebrated in a grand manner on the fixed day. The king confered many boons upon the people. The united lovers revelled in great pleasure
VII
There was a pleasant park behind the palace. In the park was there a beautiful pool/ with blooming lotuses. The lovers were there, speaking about their love, their dreams and so on. The princess requested him to sing a song. He sang a song in which he said that the pure love of the princedts  alone did make him a reformed man.
Then Komalavalli also sang a song. She sang “Oh my dear! you’ve robbed my heart. I cannot bear even a little while of parting. So you’ve n’t left your old profession of robbery”
But the old robber could not catch the meaning. He got his feelings lacerated. He got offended. But he did not show out his anger. Then they left the park and went to the palace.
VIII
From then onwards the robber got perplexed in mind. thoughts of his old guilt tormented him. He found suspicion in the eyes of the others whenever they locked at him. He was haunted by an ever-growing sense of guilt. The royal life became sour to him. He began to hate Komalavalli. He awaited a chance to take vengence upon her.
The Summer season also had come. The princess got inflammation of skin owing to excessive heat of the climate. Nathan thought that apt chance had come to execute his vengence. He proposed that they may go to Nilgiris where it would be pleasant to be in the summer season. She also accepted.
They reached Nilgiri with their attendants. They settled in a palace. Many days glided by. It was a newmoon day. On that evening they reached a mount. Below the mount was a deep canyon. It became dark. The princess proposed that they may go to the palace. But the thief said “you humiliated me. You called me a thief your vanity. I am going to kill you. You now itself pray your god.”
The princess thought in her mind that she would kill the trecherous robber. So she said to the thief, “you are my Deity. I do not pray any god but you. Please stand up. I will come around you thrice and pray you.”
The robber also stood up. She came behind as on the pretence of prayer and pushed him from behind. He was thrown into the deep canyon and was elimenated for ever.
But the princess could not bear the sorrow of it. She began to cry. The attendants found her in that state. They took her to the palace. The next day they left for Tanjore.
The city was plunged in grief. The people were all in sympathy for the pitiful plight of the princess. The aged king also became fed up with his kingly status. He convened the court and declared “My dear citizens! I have grown old! The Princess became the most pitiable woman because she married a robber who is to rule this country. We shall like to know your opinion.”
The chief minister argued “Komalavalli may ascend the throne. Her act of killing the thief was an act of courage. It is no sin.” All the courtiers accepted the view.
The king stared interrogatively at her face. She spoke “I am doomed because I did belive in false love. “The mouth of a furnace can be sealed but  one cannot shut the mouth of the gossipping world. Moreover, people’s government is the best government. People can rule themselves in a better way than we rule. So let this land become a peoples land, a republic.”
The king and other accepted her words. The king expressed his wish that he would be one of the citizens; helping the rule of the poeples republic. Komalavalli also nodded her assent to be a citizen helping the republic to thrive, the republic of the great Tamils.
The people formed in a great republic; elected a repreentative assemply, made one among them the chief and lived very happily thence forward.

ettaṉaiyō vīrar irāppakalāyc cuṟṟiṉār
attaṉaikkum taṇṭāmal vāḻntiruntāṉ āṇmaiyuḷāṉ.
iṉṉavaṉai iṉṟē piṭittāṉ iruḷappaṉ.
maṉṉaṉ maṉamakiḻvāṉ; māṇap paricaḷippāṉ;
kāvalaril muṉṉavaṉāyk kāṇum paṭivaippāṉ;
yāvarum pōṟṟip pukaḻvār ivaṉtiṟattai
ipperumai eṅkaṭku vāyttilatē! emmītu
tappillai; yāmcey tavakkuṟaivē! eṉṉap
poṟāmai maṉatteḻunta vīrar pukaṉṟārkaḷ
aṟāta tuyaruṭaṉē ceṉṟārkaḷ aṅkorupāl.
 viḷaiyāṭṭup piḷḷaikaḷ viḷampuvatu
taṅkattāṟ ceyta patumai taraiyuruṇṭu
poṅkum puḻuti uṭalpūci niṟpatupōl
aṅkam muḻutum aḻukkāl oḷikuṟaintār;
taṅkāmal ōriṭattum tāṇṭik kutitteḻuntār.
ōṭi viḷaiyāṭi ōyviṉṟic caṇṭaiyiṭṭuk
kūṭik kulāvum kuḻantaikaḷ; kūṭṭamāy
kūraiyilum, māṭiyilum, kōṭṭu maraṅkaḷilum,
ēṟik kuraṅkiṉam pōliruntu nōkkiṉār;
appāvō! ittiruṭaṉ iṉṟē akappaṭṭāṉ
eppōtum innakaram pēcum ivaṉpēccē!
āḻakkaṇ vāykkaraiyil ārum pukamuṭiyāt 
tāḻam putarnaṭuvē taṅki maṟaintāṉām!
icceyti kaṇṭāṉ; iruḷappaṉ namvīraṉ;
accam ciṟitaṟiyāṉ akkāṭṭiṉ uṭpukuntāṉ;
kaṭṭik koṇarntuviṭṭāṉ; kāṇpīr avaṉmukattai
tuṭṭat taṉamellām tuḷḷik kutippaṉavē!
keṭṭa payalivaṉpērk kēṭṭatum nammaracaṉ
veṭṭit talaivīḻtta āṇai viraintaḷippāṉ;
viṭṭuviṭa māṭṭāṉ; viruppamuṭaṉ nāmiṉimēl
veṭṭa veḷiccattum, veṇṇilāp pōtiṉilum,
appakkam ceṉṟunām āṭiviḷai yāṭalām
eppoḻutum eṉṟār iṉitu.
	cāṉṟōr kaṇṭu varuntal
vāṭum payirkku maḻaiyaip pōl - keṭṭa
vaṟumai arakkaṉāl tuṉpuṟum - makkaḷ
nāṭum cukaṅkaḷ peṟumpaṭi - inta
nāṭṭil paṇicey tuvāḻntilaṉ - ivaṉ
kēṭu maṉaṅkoṇṭu ceytatāl - intak
kīḻ nilaikkāḷā kiviṭṭaṉaṉ - eṉṟu
kēṭum aṟivuṭai māntarkaḷ - antat
tīraṉaik kaṇṭu moḻintaṉar.
nammai maṉattil nāṭuvōr - atai
nāḷum purintu makiḻuvār - entat
tiṉmai yavaraic cūḻiṉum - caṟṟum 
tīram kuṟaintiṭal illaikāṇ - perun
tammai ivaṉkai viṭuttaṉaṉ - veṟun
taṟutalai yākit tirintiṭṭāṉ- ivaṉ
taṉmai yaḻakum payaṉiṉṟi - vīḻntu
vāṭiya pūviṉaip pōṉṟavē.
muḷḷup putarniṟai kāṭṭuḷḷē - oru
mutirnta kaṉimaram niṉṟiṭṭāl - ataik
koḷḷak kuṟukuvar yāruḷḷār - intak
kōpak kuṟikoṇṭa vālipaṉ - tuṭṭak
kuḷḷa maṉitarkaḷ cērkkaiyāl - nalla
kūṭṭam peṟak keṭṭaṉaṉ - itil
eḷḷaḷavum aiyam illaikāṇ - eṉṟu
iyampi niṉṟaṉar varuntiyē.
innilattil vāḻviṉṟi eṉṟum perumtuyaril
maṉṉuvōr tuṉpamelām māṇṭu maṟaintoḻiya
nallaṟaṅkaḷ ceytiruntāl, nāḷpalavāyc cūriyaṉṟaṉ
pollāk kaṭuveyilāl  poṉṟum nilaiyaṭainta
nelliṉ payirum nilaiyaḻinta puṟpūṇṭum
cilleṉṟu mēkam ceṟintu maḻaipoḻiyat
taṅkaḷ tuyaram tavirntu talainimirntē
eṅkum pacumai eḻilviḷaṅka niṟpatupōl
āṉanta māki aṉaivōrum vāḻttuvār.
īṉaṉ eṉakkūṟi eḷḷuvār yārumilar;
ēḻaik kuṭimakkaḷ iṉpam peṟavuraittal
pāḻuṭampai nalluṭampāyp paṇṇum vaḻi, inta
uṇmaiyaik kāṇāṉ, ulakōr paḻimoḻiyat
tiṇmai uṭaiyāṉ tiruṭaṉ kolaiñaṉāy
vāḻntāṉ; aṟattiṉ vaḻiyaṟiyāṉ; eṉṟu tuyar 
āḻntu palacolli niṉṟār aṟivuṭaiyōr.
peṇkaḷ pēccu
ucciyilē kārmēkam vāḻa uṭampiṉilē
paccaic civappumayil mañcaṇiṟap paṭṭuṭuttik
kātilē tōṭaṇintu kaiyil vaḷaitarittu
vīti viḷakkēpōl vīcum paṇipūṇṭu
miṉṉal koṭipalavum mētiṉiyil vantulavik
kaṉṉal moḻipeytu kātaṟ payirvaḷarttu
niṉṟu naṭappaveṉa, nēriḻaiyār paṟpalarum 
oṉṟu kuḻumiṉār; oṇpuruva villiṉilē
kaṇṇampu pūṭṭik kaṭiteṟintār; vañcaṉuṭal
vaṇṇattaik kaṇṭu vaḷaikōtta kaikaḷaittam
kaṉṉattil ūṉṟiṉār; kaṇṇimaippu nīkkiṉār;
eṉṉaṭi! āyi ! ivaṉumoru kaḷvaṉō!
cittirattum kāṇac ciṟanta vaṭivaḻakaṉ!
itkaiya vīraṉ iḻitoḻilaic ceyvāṉō!
pūraṇac cantiraṉaip pōṉṟa mukamuṭaiyāṉ!
māraṉum maṇṭiyiṭṭu maṉṟāṭum pēraḻakaṉ!
pālpōl veḷutta pallaḻakuk kāraṉivaṉ!
cēlpōl viḻiyuṭaiyār cintai kuṭipukuvāṉ!
eṉṟu palarpēca iḷaiyāḷ orumaṅkai
naṉṟu pukaṉṟīrnīr nāṭṭu naṭappaṟiyīr!
teyvapattik kāṉa tiruvēṭam koṇṭavarkaḷ 
vaiyakattil pātakarāy vāḻuvataik kāṇīrō!
nāṭṭiṉ nalattukkē nāṅkaḷ uḻaikkiṉṟōm!
kēṭṭai aḻikkumuṉ kīḻppaṭiyōm ! yārkkumeṉak
kūṭṭattil niṉṟu kutittup palapēci
vīṭṭukkuḷ vēṟupala ceytirukkum vīṇarainīr!
kaṇṭum aṟiyīrō! kēṭṭum uṇarīrō!
uṇṭō umatakattil uṇmai teriyumoḷi?
nalla marakatampōl kaṇkavarum nañciṉilē
kollum koṭumai kuṭiyiruttal kāṇīrō!
kaṇṇāṭi pōlak kaviḻntu mukampārkka
oṇṇum teḷinīriṉ uḷḷē palanañcup
pūccikaḷum vāḻntu pucippōrai māykkiṉṟa
ācciriyam nīṅkaḷ aṟiyīrō! taṅkakaiḷ!
taṅka vilaimatippait tāḻvākac ceyyulakam 
aṅkataṉiṟ cērntirukkum ceyti aṟiyīrō!
vāca malarccōlai vāḻntuvarum teṉṟaluṭaṉ
nāca viṭakkāṟṟuc cērntu naliyātō!
ātalāl nīṅkaḷ aḻakāl mayaṅkātīr!
mātarīr! coṉṉa maṭamai moḻimaṟappīr!
eṉṉak kuyiṟkulattār uṇmaiyivaḷ colleṉṟē
paṉṉi metuvākap pāvaiyarum ceṉṟārkaḷ.
tōḻarkaḷ ceykai
kūṭṭāḷik kaḷvar kuḻuviṉ naṭuviṉilē
kāṭṭāmal tammuruvaik kaṇkalaṅkic ceṉṟārkaḷ;
taṅkaḷ talaivaṉait tappivikkac cintittār
eṅkeṅkum kāvalarkaḷ eccarikkai yāyccūḻntu 
celluvataṉā loṉṟum ceyya vaḻiyiṉṟi
mella naḻuviviṭṭār mikka tuyaruṭaṉē.
iḷavaraciyum kaḷvaṉum oruvaraiyoruvar kāṇutal
inta vakaiyāka ellālōrum cūḻntuvara
antap peruvīraṉ taṉṉai araṇmaṉaiyiṉ
vīti vaḻiyāka viraintiḻuttu vantārkaḷ;
ōtum peruviḻāvil ōṅkum olipōṉṟa
makkaḷ kuralōcai maṉṉaṉ makaḷkēṭṭāḷ;
ekkā raṇaminta ōcaikku eṉaniṉaintāḷ;
cēṭiyar kūṭṭamuṭaṉ cērntuviḷai yāṭiyavaḷ
ōṭiyē vantuṟṟāḷ upparikai mītiṉilē
kūṭṭattai nōkkiṉāḷ kōmaḷa valliyavaḷ
vāṭṭamilāt tōṟṟam! vaḷarntapiṟai pōṉṟamukam,
ārvap payirvaḷara āṇmai maḻaipoḻiyum
mārpam uṭaiyāṉ! maṉakkiṉiya ṉāyniṉṟāṉ!
kuṉṟaṉaiya tōḷkaḷ ! kuṟaiyākkem pīranaṭai
aṉṟalarnta tāmaraiyē aṉṉa tirukkaṇkaḷ!
uḷḷāṉ ikkaṭṭaḻakaṉ! ūrkkā valarkūṭik
kaḷḷaṉaippōl kaṭṭi iḻukkiṉṟār ēṉōtāṉ?
eṉṟu palaveṇṇi imaiyāmaṟ kaṇmalaraic
ceṉṟu mukaṅkuḷirac cintiṉāḷ; cuntaraṉum
aṇṇāntu pārkka araṇmaṉaiyiṉ māṭiyilē
taṇṇāram pūṇṭirunta taiyal oruttipala
viṇmīṉ puṭaicūḻa veḷḷai nilāvīcit 
taṇmatiyam oṉṟu tavampurintu niṟpatupōl
kārikaiyāḷ niṉṟutaṉ kaṇmalarkaḷ vīcuvatai
vīraṉun tāṉkaṇṭāṉ viṭṭeṟintāṉ tāmaraiyaic
centā maraiyōṭi cevvaricūḻ vaṇṭukaḷaip 
paintēṉ aruttutaṟkup paṟṟumoru ceykaipōl
aṉṉattiṉ kaṇkaḷuṭaṉ aṉpāṟ kalantuviṭa
miṉṉal iṭaiyāḷum, meymaṟantu niṉṟu viṭṭāḷ
naṉṟāy avaṉuruvai nāṭṭiviṭṭu neñciṉilē
aṉṟē avaṉmārpil aṉpaip patittuviṭṭāḷ;
kātal perunōkkāl kaṭṭaḻakaṉ āṇmaitaṉai
 mōti aḻittavaḷum mōka valaivīḻntāḷ;
kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇiṇai nōkkokkiṉ vāyccoṟkaḷ
eṉṉa payaṉum ila veṉṟu vaḷḷuvar
coṉṉa moḻipalikkac cuntaraṉum appoḻutē
tēṅkip perumūccu viṭṭāṉ; tikaippuṟṟāḷ;
pāṅkirunta tōḻiyarum pārttār avaḷnilaiyai;
kūṭṭam maṟaiyumaṭṭum kaṇṇimaikaḷ koṭṭāmal
tīṭṭāta cittiramāy niṉṟāḷ terunōkki;
māntar tiraḷmaṟainta piṉṉar, matimukattuc
cāntaṇinta kūntalaic calippuṟṟa pārvaiyāḷiṉ 
vāṭi maṉamcōr vaṭivamatait tōḻiyarkaḷ 
vēṭikkai pēci viḷaiyāṭak koṇṭaṇaintār.
kaḷvaṉai aracaṉmuṉ koṇartal
peṟṟōrai yaṉṟip piṟaporuḷkaḷ vēṇṭuvaṉa
muṟṟāka vāṅka muṭiyum kaṭaitteruvum 
vāṉakattē yām peṟṟa vāḻvuḷavō eṉṟaṟiya 
āṉavarai yōṅki aḻakirukkum māṭaṅkaḷ
mītē mukiliruntu mikkakuḷīr kāṭṭukiṉṟa 
vīti kaḷumkaṭantu vīram niṟaintaperum
añcāta cuntara nātaṉ avaṉ taṉṉaik
koñca  nērattiṟkuḷ koṇṭaṇaintār maṉṉaṉmuṉ
maṉṉaṉ tayāniti makiḻvaṭaintāṉ appōtu;
kaṉṉeñcac cuntaraṉaik kaṭṭik koṇarntavarkkut
takka paricaḷittāṉ; tāḻāmal nīrivaṉai
ikkaṇamē koṇṭu ciṟaikkuḷ iruttiṭuvīr!
nāḷaip pakaṟpōtil nammuṭaiya maṉṟattil 
nīḷa vicarittu nītimuṟai ceyvōm! 
eṉṉa moḻintaṉpiṉ ellōrum pōyviṭṭār.
maṇṇuḷōr neñcam kalaṅki mayakkamuṟap
paṇṇum perunōyaip paṟṟip piṇittāṟpōl
aṉṉava ṉaicciṟaiyil taḷḷi aṭaittār;
iravu viṭiyumaṭṭum kāvalarkaḷ ellām
uṟaṅkāmal kāttār oruṅku.

karukiya kañcam
añcukattai maṉṉaṉ aḷitta cuṭarviḷakkai
neñca valaippaṭutti niṉṟatoru cuntaraṉum
tūṅkāṉ viḻittiruntāṉ; tukkak kaṭalpaṭintāṉ;
āṅkē tāṉpārtta aḻakāṉa peṇmayilai
eṇṇiyeṇṇi iṉpuṟuvāṉ; ēṅkuvāṉ neñcākum
kaṇṇiṉāṟ kaṇṭu kalaṅkuvāṉ; cintippāṉ;
kaḷvaṉ maṉakkātal
pārulakikal nāṉkaṇṭa pāvaiyarkaḷ paṟpalapēr
ārumeṉ neñciṉilē añcāmal vantatilai;
ettaṉaiyō maṅkaimār eṉṉai maṇappataṟkuc
cittattil eṇṇiyeṉaic cērnturaitta kālamelām
caṟṟum umaivirumpēṉ ! nāṉmaṇakkac cammatiyēṉ
muṟṟum veṟukkiṉṟēṉ! moykuḻalīr! eṉṟuraittēṉ!
veppattiṟ paṭṭurukum veṇṇeyyaip pōluḷḷam
ippaṭi nainturukac ceytāḷ, eḻilaraci!
upparikai mēlniṉ ṟorupūṅ koṭiyeṉmēl
oppariya nīla malarai utirttuviṭṭāḷ!
annīlap pūvaināṉ āvalāyk kaṇvaḻiyē
eṉṉeñciṉ uḷvaittēṉ! iṉpuṟṟēṉ! eṉceyvēṉ!
kollumvilaṅku kuṭiyirukkum kāṭukaḷil
cellum poḻutumnāṉ cintai aḻintatilai;
niṉṟukīḻ nōkkiṉ nilaikalaṅkac ceykiṉṟa
kuṉṟiṉmēl ēṟik kutittapō tum añcēṉ!
veḷḷaṅ karaipuraṇṭu vīḻap palamaraṅkaḷ 
taḷḷi aṇaiyuṭaittut  tāṅkāmal ōṭum 
natikaḷait tāṇṭutaṟkum nāṉ ciṟitum añcēṉ
putiyavaḻi muṭputarkkuḷ pōvēṉ tuṇivuṭaṉē!
kaṇkoṇṭu pārttāl kalakkamuṟum kāriruḷil
maṇmuḻutum cuṟṟi makiḻvēṉ! maṉaṅkalaṅkēṉ!
ciṅkam pulikaḷeṉaic cīṟi etirttālum
aṅkē taṭuppēṉ! aḻippēṉ avaimāḷa!
vīrāti vīrarelām vīḻntē taraipuraḷat 
tīramuṭaṉ pōrceyvēṉ! tīrppēṉ avaruṭalai!
uḷḷamum pēccum orutaṉmai yillāmal
kaḷḷat taṉañcey kayavaraik kaṇṭālum
eḷḷat taṉaikūṭa añcā(tu) etirttavaraip
paḷḷattil vīḻum paṭiveṟṟi peṟṟiṭuvēṉ
ekkālum cōrāta taṉmaiyuḷa eṉṉeñcam 
ikkālam vāṭi irukkum tiṟamaṟiyēṉ!
kaṉṉikaiyiṉ vēlviḻiyum kaṭṭaḻakum cērnteṉṉai
innilaiyil vaittaṉavē āā! ituviyappē!
ā! veṉ matiyirunta vāṟeṉ? aṟivillēṉ! 
pāvi avaḷivvūrp pārttipaṉ celvitāṉ!
eṭṭāta māṅkaṉiyai eyta oruciṟuvaṉ
koṭṭāvi pōkkalpōl āṉēṉ! koṭiyaṉēṉ!
vāṉat tiḷampiṟaiyai vāveṉ ṟaḻaittaḻutu
kāṉal kuralkāṭṭum kaikkuḻantai pōlāṉēṉ!
pañcavar ṇakkiḷiyaip pārppataṟkuk kaṇṇillāṉ
koñcik koṭuveṉṟu kēṭpatāl eṉṉapayaṉ?
vāyillā maṅkai vaḷarkītam kaṟpataṉāl
āya palaṉeṉṉa? aṅkōr malaimukaṭṭil
centēṉ aṭaipiḷantu cinta ataivirumpi
nontutaṉ nānīṭṭum noṇṭi peṟumpayaṉeṉ?
inta vitamellām eṇṇi avaṉimaippil
cintai paṟipōkac ceytirunta nērattē!
māṉēr viḻikkumari maṅkai malaraṇintu
tāṉē avaṉetiril puṉmuṟuval tantirukka,
maṉṉaṉ makaḷvantāḷ eṉṟu maruṇṭavaṉāyc
coṉṉāṉ palamoḻikaḷ cōrntu
kaḷvaṉ kūṟutal 
cintu
neñcil kuṭipukuntāy - nilai
niṉṟu kaṇkāṭṭi mayakkiṉai māṉē!
añcum viṉaipurintēṉ! - iṉi
attoḻil ceytilēṉ! viṭṭaṉeṉ āṇai!
nañcāy eṉai varuttēl !- oru
naṉmoḻi tantuyir kāttaruḷ vāynī!
vañci yiṇaiviḻiyāl - maṉa
vallamai tīrntu maṟaintatu mātē!
paṭṭiṉi vātaiyiṉāl  - intap 
pāriṉil tuṉpam piṇikoṇṭu tavippār
kiṭṭiya nalluṇavāl - neñcam 
kiḷarntu makiḻntu kaḷippuṟal pōlē
kaṭṭik karumpuṉaiyē - nāṉ
kaṇṭatum kātal koṇṭu makiḻntēṉ;
maṭṭit taṉamataṉāl - inta
mānila mītiṉi tītukaḷ ceyyēṉ;
kātal taṉaiyitunāḷ - varaik 
kaṇṭaṟiyēṉ; collak kēṭṭatēyaṉṟik,
kōtai mārpalarāl - maṉam 
koḷḷai koḷḷāmaṟ piḻaittaṉeṉ! innāḷ,
mātē! uṉataḻakāl -iṉṟu
maṉaṅkavar kātal ituveṉṟu kaṇṭēṉ!
ētō ituvaṟiyēṉ! nī
eṉuḷḷam koṇṭaṉai ! vīḻntaṉeṉ uṉpāl!
maṉṉaṉ maṭamakaḷē! - oḷir
matimukaṅ koṇṭeṉ maṉattuḷ nuḻaintē
eṉṉaip putumaiyilē - nī
iruttiṉai pittaṉpōl āyiṉeṉ niṉṟēṉ
uṉṉāl kuṟaiyaṟintēṉ - iṉi
oruvakait tīmaiyum ceytiṭa māṭṭēṉ!
kaṇṇē! iṉi varuttēl! - eṉṟaṉ
kaiyil aṇaintaruḷ! kaṇkavar tēvi!
(kaliveṇpā)
kaḷḷaṉiv vāṟuraittuk kārikaiyaik kaippiṭikka
meḷḷa veḻuttiruntāṉ; miṉṉaṟ koṭimaṟaintāḷ;
uḷḷattē uṇmai uṇarāta tālavaṉum 
tuḷḷik kutittāṉ; tuḻāviṉāṉ aṅkellām;
uḷḷattāl eṇṇum uruvam kaṉaviṉilē
meḷḷa veḷippaṭum; meymai avaṉaṟiyāṉ
aṉṉāṉ maṉattuḷḷē vāḻnta avaḷuruvē
kaṇṇiṉ etirtōṉṟik kāṭci aḷittateṉak
kaṇṭaṟi yāmal kataikaḷ palacollit 
tiṇṭāṭi niṉṟāḷ tiruṭaṉ; atukālai,
kāvalar kaṭital
kāvalarkaḷ aṉṉavaṉaik kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟār;
āvā! ituveṉ ! aṟiyōm! uḷaṟal!
ciṟaiyuḷ irukkumivaṉ cintai kalaṅki 
aṟaivāṉ! ētētō; aiyamilai paittiyamē!
eṉṟavarkaḷ pēci ēṇṭā uḷaṟukiṟāy!
naṉṟaṟiyāk kaḷvā! pitaṟṟātē! naṉṟāy
uṟaṅkuvāy ! nāḷai uyirkavar vāṉmaṉṉaṉ
maṟaintu viṭumuṉṟaṉ maṉattuyaram eṉṟārkaḷ
kaḷvaṉ viṭai
maṉṉavaṉ peṇpāl maṉattai aṭakuvaitta
aṉṉavaṉ,pētaikāḷ! āynteṉ nilaiyaṟiyīr!
pittaṉeṉ ṟētētō pēci irāmuḻutum
antac ciṟaikkuḷ iruntārkaḷ. avviravil
neñcam paṟikoṭutta nēriḻaiyāḷ paṭṭatuyar
koñcamala vellām kūṟuvataṟ kārvallār?
aracakumāriyiṉ tuṉpam
nīlat tukilvirittu nittilaṅkaḷ mēḷparappik
kōlañcey veḷḷik kuṭamoṉṟu vaittatupōl
ūṭalilē cōrntirunta kātalarkaḷ uḷḷamalar
kūṭalilē cellak kumutam itaḻviriya,
veḷḷai nilāccinti, veṅkatirāl tuṉpuṟṟōr
uḷḷamakiḻ veyta utittāṉ mativāṉil;
vanta matikaṇṭu vāḻttiṉār kātalarkaḷ;
inta nilavamutai ellōrum uṇṭiruntār;
kaḷvaṉiṭam neñcu paṟipōṉa kārikaitāṉ
oḷvaḷaikaḷ cōra, uṭampellām tīccūḻa,
nāṭṭiyam ceypatumai nāṇaṟṟu vīḻntateṉa
āṭṭa melāmviṭṭāḷ; amaḷiyiṉ mēṟkiṭantāḷ;
kāykkumoru māṅkaṉṟaik kattiyiṉāl veṭṭic
cāytta teṉakkiṭantāḷ; cāmpiṉāḷ; tēmpiṉāḷ;
nalla malarkkoṭiyai nācamuṟa vērōṭu
kallik kaṭuveyiliṟ kāya viṭuttateṉa
mullai muṟuvalāḷ cōrntāḷ mukamalartāṉ;
kollaimāṉ neñcam kumuṟiṉāḷ; vāṭiṉāḷ;
kañca malaroṉṟu karukik kiṭantateṉap 
pañcaṇaiyil vīḻntu paṭuttiruntāḷ; pāvainilai
kaṇṭatuṇait tōḻimār kaṇmalarkaḷ; mūṭavilai;
uṇṭāḷō pōtai! uṇarcci maṟantateṉ?
ētō mayakkuṟṟāḷ; collāḷ emakkoṉṟum;
tītō ivaḷuṭaṟkē! tēṟōm! eṉappulampik
kollaṉulaikkāṟṟēpōl kōtaimār mūccuyirttār;
alli malarkkoṭiyai āṟṟāta veyyililē
kiḷḷi yeṟiyak kiṭantu tuvaḷvatupōl
koḷḷaic cirippuṭaiya kovvai yitaḻāḷum
cērnta paṭukkaiyilē cintai kalaṅkimukam 
cōrntu kiṭantāḷ cuḻaṉṟu.

ācciriyap pēriruḷ
( kaliveṇpā)
nīlaniṟak kampaḷiyai nīṇilattiṉ mēlvirittuk 
kōlap pacumpoṉ kuṭamniṟutti vaittatupōl
kālaik katirōṉ kaṭalmukattil tōṉṟumuṉ,
cōlaip paṟavaiyelām cōrviṉṟik kokkarittuk 
kūṭik kaḷittuk kutūkala māyiruppap
pāṭum paṟavaiyiṉam pāṭi makiḻntirukka,
vaṉṉac ciṟaikkulaṅkaḷ vārip pucippataṟkē
aṉṉattai yeṅkum aḷḷi yiṟaittāṟpōl
mullai malarum muṟukkaviḻnta mallikaiyum 
ellaiyilā vācaṉaiyō ṭeṅkum malarntirukka,
koṇṭāṉ kotipṭaintu kūṟum moḻittīyāl
cuṇṭum maṉaivimukam pōlavē cōrvāki
alli malarkuviya; aṉpāyp palapēcip
pulla varumkoḻunaṉ puṉṉakaiyāl neñciṉilē
tuḷḷum makiḻccimukam tōṉṟuvatu pōlmaṉattai
aḷḷum taṭantōṟum tāmaraikaḷ vāyaviḻap;
paḷḷic ciṟuvarelām pāṭam paṭippataṟkut
tuḷḷi yeḻuntirun tuṭkārntu tūṅkiviḻa
vēlaikkuc celvōr virainteḻuntu tattamatu
kālaik kaṭaṉkaḷ kaṭitākac ceytiruppat
tūkkam viḻitteḻuntu tōkaiyarkaḷ ellōrum
ūkkattai āṭavarum kūṭṭum mukantirutti
illattu vēlaikaḷaic ceytirukkum ellaiyilē,
ellōrum kāṇa iḷampariti tōṉṟiṉāṉ.
poṅkum neruppāyp polikiṉṟa cūriyaṉum
eṅkeṅkum taṉṉoḷiyai ēṟap parappiṉāṉ.
avvoḷiyiṉ veḷḷattil āḻnta poruḷellām
cevvaiyāyp poṉcālvai pōrttic ciṟantaṉavē!
tōṭṭa malarkaḷ toṭaiyaviḻntu vaṇṭukaḷiṉ
kūṭṭaṅ kaḷaikkūvit tēṉaruttik koṇṭirukkum;
innērap pōtilav vūril iruppavarkaḷ
muṉṉāḷ nikaḻcci mutalil niṉaittārkaḷ;
añcāta ciṅkaṉ vaḻakkai namataracaṉ
cuntara nātaṉeṉum cōraṉ peruvaḻakkai
intanāḷ nammaracaṉ kēṭpāṉ, kolaiceyvāṉ
iññāṉṟu kēṭpāṉ; iṭuvāṉ kolaittaṇṭam;
nāmuṉṉē ceṉṟanta naṉṉikaḻcci kāṇuvōm!
pōmeṉṟu yārnammait taḷḷiṉum pōkōm!
eṉappēci māntarellām ērāḷa māka
vaṉappāṉa maṉṉaṉ capainōkki vantiruntār.
kaḷvaṉ vaḻakku
karantainakar vēntaṉ kalaṅkāta neñcaṉ
aṟantavaṟā malneṟiyil āḷum aṟivuṭaiyōṉ
aṉṉāṉ orucelvam āṭcik kuriyamakaḷ
poṉṉē eṉakkaṇṭār pōṟṟum eḻiṟkoḻuntu
kōmaḷa valliyeṉak kūṟum peyaruṭaiyāḷ,
kāmattaip pāyccum kaṇṇuṭaiyāḷ, cevvitaḻāḷ;
nīril nimirntu niṟkum malarmukattāḷ;
pārap peruñcuvaicēr paintamiḻiṉ cēmaniti;
nūlpalavum kaṟṟaṟintāḷ; nuṭpa matiyuṭaiyāḷ;
pālpōṉṟa veṇmaṉattāḷ; pāviṉ cuvai nukarvāḷ;
nītinūṟ koḷkai yelām naṉkaṟivāḷ, nīḷkuḻalāḷ;
ōtuvatiṟ kālattai ōyāmaṟ pōkkuvāḷ.
aṉṉavaḷ pālaracaṉ ārā viruppuṭaiyaṉ;
eṉṉaceya lēṉum ivaḷiṭattiṟ collāmal
ceyvatilai ātālāl, centamiḻk kaṉṉiyavaḷ
vaikum iṭantēṭi vantu muṭimaṉṉaṉ,
mātar kulakkoḻuntē! nāḷainam mācapaiyil
tītupala ōruruvāyt tiraṇṭeḻun tāṟpōṉṟōṉ
cūtilē vallavaṉ cuntara nātaṉeṉum 
kātakaṉ kaḷvaṉ kaṭuñcintai yāḷaṉmēl
vaḻakku naṭakkum vakaikāṇa nīyum 
paḻakka muḷatōḻi yuṭaṉvantu pārttiruppāy!
paṭṭat tiḷavaraci yātalāṟ pārāḷum 
caṭṭatiṭ ṭaṅkaḷ cariyākak kaṇṭuṇarvāy!
eṉṟuraittāṉ. maṉṉavaṉum ēṟṟa uṭaipūṇṭu
maṉṟam pukuntāṉ; matiyamaiccar pōṟṟiṉār;
ūrārkaḷ ellōrum uḷḷak kaḷippuṭaṉē
pārāḷum vēntaṉai vāḻttip paṇintārkaḷ;
ārā amutaṉaiyāḷ aṅkōr puṟattamarntu
māraṉuk kōlai celaviṭṭu makiḻntiruntāḷ.
kāvalarkaḷ avviya kaḷvaṉaik kaṭṭuṭaṉē,
āvaluṭaṉ makkaḷ aṉaivōrum pārkka
neruṅki yiṭittu viḻipituṅki niṟkak,
karuṅkal maṉakkaḷvaṉ taṉṉaik, kalaimaṉṉaṉ
maṉṟattē yīrttuk koṇarntu, maṉañcaliyā
teṉṟum paṇipurivōr vaittār, etirniṟutti.
appoḻutu maṉṉaṉ avaṉmuṉ purintapala
tappitaṅkaḷ kāṭṭuvāṉ tāṉ.
aracaṉ kūṟutul
(āciriya viruttam)
ūrāriṉ maṉampataikkat tīmai ceytāy!
	oruvarukkum teriyamal oḷinti runtāy!
ērāḷa māyuḻaittu varunti nāḷum 
	iṉpuṟavē makkaḷelām celvam cērppār;
ōrāmal avaikaḷainī tiruṭic ceṉṟē
	uṉcukattaip pēṇutalōr kuṟṟam aṉṟē?
cīrāṉa naṉṉeṟikaḷ kaṭaippi ṭittē
	ciṟantavaṉāy vāḻumaṟi vuṉakkēṉ illai?
pāṭupaṭṭiṅ kuḻaippataṟkē kāluṅ kaiyum
	palamāṉa kaṭṭuṭalum peṟṟi ruppāy!
nāṭuviṭṭu nāṭōṭip palaru ḻaittē
	nalvāḻvu naṭattuvatum kaṇṭu niṉṟāy !
kāṭutaṉil viṟakoṭittu viṟṟu vāḻiṉ 
	kaṇṭavarkaḷ uṉaiyikaḻār aṉṟō colka!
tēṭutaṟku muṭiyāta māṉan taṉṉait 
	tiruṭaṉeṉum paḻipūṇṭu viṭṭu viṭṭāy!
potumakkaḷ matikkumoru toḻilaic ceytu
	pukaḻāka vāḻvataṟkām aṟivē yiṉṟip
patitarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭattait tuṇaiyāk koṇṭāy!
	pālarkaḷum mātarkaḷum naṭukkam koḷḷa
atikoṭiya ceyalkaḷilē īṭu paṭṭāy!
	aḻakiyavuṉ kaṭṭiḷamai aḻittu viṭṭāy!
matuvarunti matikeṭṭut tirintu niṉṟāy!
	maṉitaṉeṉa uṉaiyiṅku matippār uṇṭō!
kaḷḷaviṉait toḻilpurintu vāḻntu vantāy
	kayavaṉē! ivvaḷavō uṉatu kuṟṟam !
meḷḷaevar coṉṉālum accol kēṭpiṉ
	mēṉiyelām vitirvitirttu vērvai yāki
uḷḷamelām cōrvaṭaiyum kolaiyaic ceyya
	orupoḻutum añcāta valiya neñcā!
koḷḷaiyuyir koṉṟulaka kāla ṉākik
	kolaipputariṉ uḷvāḻnta koṭiya tīyā!
piṭittuṉṉaik koṇṭuvarak kāval vīrar 
	pirintupala ticaikaḷilum tēṭip pārttār;
taṭittaṉattāl avarkaiyil akappaṭāmal
	tappittu maṟaivākat tirintu vāḻntāy!
kuṭittaṉattār palaruṉṉāl tuṉpam eyta
	koṭuṅkōlkoḷ maṉṉavaṉāyk kuṟumpu ceytāy!
aṭittiṅku viraṭṭiviṭa māṭṭēṉ uṉṟaṉ
	aṭātapaḻik kēṟṟavoru taṇṭam ceyvēṉ!  
	kaḷvaṉ vēṇṭukōḷum
aracaṉ viṭaiyum 
(paḵṟoṭai veṇpā)
vēntaṉ uraikēṭṭu vīṟṟirunta mantirimār,
māntar palarumoru māṟṟamuraiyā tiruntār.
añcāta ciṅkaṉ aracaṉmuka nōkkik
cōrā mukamuṭaiya cuntaranātaṉ avaṉ
nērē nimirntaṅku niṉṟu moḻintiṭṭāṉ;
koñcamum cōrvilāṉ kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ;
pūmaṇ ṭalamāḷum vēntē! pukaḻuṭaiyōy!
yāmumoru viṇṇappam ceyvōm! emakkunī
uttaravu tantāl uraikkiṉṟōm iccapaiyiṉ 
mattiyilē eṉṟavaṉ māṟṟumoḻi kūṟiṉāṉ;
evvuraikaḷ kūṟanī eṇṇiṭiṉum ippoḻutē
avvuraikaḷ collalām añcāmal eṉṟanta
maṉṉaṉ viṭaitantāṉ; māvīraṉ cuntaraṉum
coṉṉāṉ palamoḻikaḷ cūḻntu.
kaḷvaṉ moḻi
eṉṉaiyoru tiruṭaṉeṉa muṭivu ceytāy,
	iḻivākap palamoḻikaḷ pēci viṭṭāy!
aṉṉaiyiṉun tayavuṭaiyāṉ nīyeṉ ṟeṇṇi
	aṟiyātār uṉpātam tāṅki niṉṟār!
uṉṉaiyumnāṉ tiruṭaṉeṉak kūṟu kiṉṟēṉ!
	uṇmai yeṉṟē meyppippēṉ uṟutiyāka!
maṉṉippāy eṉṉuraiyai aracar ēṟē!
	maṭamaiyiṉāl aḻivaḻakkuc coṉṉēṉ allēṉ!
kolaikāraṉ eṉaveṉṉai ikaḻntu māntar
	kūṭṭamelām parikacikka vacaimo ḻintāy!
nilaiyāka eṉatāvi kāppa taṟkē
	niṉṟeṉaiyār etirtālum aḻitti ruppēṉ!
alaicūḻum kaṭalulakil maṉṉar tammāl
	āruyirkaḷ aḻivataṟkōr aḷavum uṇṭō!
malaiyāmal eṉmoḻiyai eṇṇip pārnī!
	maṉṉavaṉē kolaiñaṉnāṉ oruvaṉ tāṉō?
aracaṉ ciṉam
ivvuraikaḷ cuntara nātaṉ iyampiyapiṉ 
kovvaiyeṉak kaṇcivantu kōpam kuṭikoḷḷa
mīcai tuṭituṭikka, mēluntaṉ mārpiṉilē
vīciyapal kūrampu pāyntāl eṉavekuṇṭu,
vēntaṉ eḻuntiruntu viṇṇatirak kūviṉāṉ;
cāntattai māyttāṉ; caṭacaṭaṭeṉap pecukiṉṟāṉ;
ēṭā! kolaikkaḷvā! uṉṉāṇmai evvaḷavu?
tēṭāp perumpaḻikkuṉ tēkam paṟikoṭuttāy!
nāṭṭukku maṉṉavaṉai nākkup puraḷumaṭṭum
kēṭṭukkuḷ āṉamoḻi pēciṉāy kīḻmakaṉē!
tēcak kuṭimakkaḷ tēmpit tiriyāmal 
vācam purivataṟkē vāḻum aracaṉiṭam
nēcattaik kāṭṭāmal nītāṉ paḻittāy ! uṉ
mōcattai ūrār muḻutum aṟivār kaḷ ! 
eṉṉaiyumuṉ tōḻaṉeṉak kūṟi etirmoḻintāy
uṉṉait talaivīḻtti ōṭṭukiṟēṉ pār! eṉṟu
maṉṉaṉ kaṭukaṭuttāṉ; maṉṟattār accamuṟat 
taṉṉuṭaiya vāṭpaṭaiyaik kaikkoṇṭu tāṇṭiṉāṉ;
maṉṟattār ellōrum maṇpatumai yāṉārkaḷ;
ceṉṟōṭi ṉāṉmaṉṉaṉ; ceṅkarattu vāḷpaṟṟi,
mantiri āymatiyaṉ maṉṟāṭik kūṟukiṉṟāṉ,
centamiḻ vallaṉē! cīṟṟam taṇintiṭuvāy!
ippō tivaṉkūṟum īṉap poruḷmoḻikaḷ
eppaṭittāṉ okkumeṉak kēṭpōm ivaṉaiyē!
eṉṟu moḻintavuṭaṉ vāḷkoṇṭa ēntaluntāṉ 
niṉṟāṉ ciṉantaṇintu, nīrviṭṭa tīyāki;
āṇṭirunta kaḷvaṉ añcāmukam nōkki,
ēṇṭā! talaikkiṟukkā! ippōtu nīcoṉṉa
vīṇmoḻiyai naṉṟāy viḷakki uraittāṟpiṉ
kāṇkiṉṟēṉ uṉṉuṭaiya kaivaricai yāvu meṉa
maṉṉaṉ ciṉantinta māṟṟam pukaṉṟavuṭaṉ,
kaṉṉeñcaṉ colvāṉ kaṭintu.
kaḷvaṉ urai
tēcattār tuyarkaḷaiya vāḻvēṉ eṉṟu
	terivitta malarmārpiṉ maṉṉā kēḷnī !
mācaṟṟa maṉamuṭaiyār ēḻai makkaḷ
	mānilattai vaḷappaṭutti viḷaiya vaippār!
vācamalarp pūñcōlai vaḷarttu vaippār!
	vāykkālkaḷ āṟukaḷum akaḻntu vaippār!
nēcattāl nīnalaṅkaḷ ceyvā yeṉṟu
	niṉaittiruntār aṟiyāta taṉmai yālē!
irattamaḻai vērvaiyeṉa nilattē cinti
	ennāḷum pāṭupaṭum makka ḷālē
peruttapala nakaraṅkaḷ, terukkaḷ, vīṭu,
	piḻaittiruntu vāḻvataṟkām uṇavup paṇṭam
turaittaṉattai naṭattutaṟkuc celva mellām
	tōṉṟivarum uṇmaiyitu maṟukka lāmō!
varuttiyavar tamaiccuraṇṭu vōrai nīṅkaḷ
	vāḻvikka ! virukkiṉṟīr payaṉum uṇṭō!
pakaliravu pārāmal uṭalai vāṭṭip
	palatoḻilum purintulakil vāḻum makkaḷ
mikavarunti iṭamillā tuṇavum iṉṟi
	melintaḻintē pataikkiṉṟār; utavi yillai;
cakamellām pañcameṉum koṭumpēy cūḻntu
	calipṭaiya makkaḷ tamaik kacakkiṉālum
takaviṉṟi varivāṅki aracu ceytal
	tarumamō! nītivaḻi naṭappa tāmō!
ennāḷum varikaḷiṉmēl varikaḷ pōṭṭē
	ēḻaikaḷiṉ viḻikalaṅkak koṭumai ceytal
maṉṉānaṟ ceykaiyō! nīti tāṉō!
	matiyuḷḷār ceyalmuyaṟṟum aṟamē tāmō!
uṉṉāṇai uraikkiṉṟēṉ, āyntu pār nī !
	oruvitattum pētamilai nāṉum nīyum!
coṉṉālnī pakaṟkoḷḷai aṭittu niṟpāy!
	cōraṉ irāk kālattil tiruṭi niṉṟēṉ!
kolaikāraṉ eṉṟeṉṉai ikaḻntē coṉṉāy!
	kūṟukiṟēṉ uṉkolai yaikkuṟittuk kēḷnī!
nilaiyāyim maṇṇulakai āḷum kātal
	niṉaippōṭu paṭaikaḷ pala cērntu vaittāy!
caliyāmal pōrceytu talaikaḷ cāyppāy!
	caḷacaḷeṉa irattanati ōṭac ceyvāy!
ulaiyāta vīraṉeṉum peyarai nāṭi
	uṭaltuṭikka uyirkoḷvāy nīyum aṉṟō!
ātaliṉāl maṉṉavaṉē! nīyum nāṉum
	āṟṟuvatu vēṟutoḻil eṉṉa veṇṇēl;
pētamilai iruvarukkuḷ eṇṇip pārttāl;
	pittuṭaiya māntaritai uṇara māṭṭār;
cātiyilē uyarvuraittut tāḻvum collic
	catipurinta tāliṅkē mayaṅki niṉṟāy!
nītiyilē pakuttaṟivai vaittu nōkkiṉ
